================================================================================
SECTION 0: AUDITOR'S EXECUTIVE BRIEFING & MANDATE
================================================================================

귀하는 Figma2Word 프로젝트의 HWPX 문서 렌더링 엔진 결함을 조사하는 독립 기술 감사관(Web Argon)입니다.

[핵심 당면 과제]
Figma2Word는 Figma 디자인을 중간 표준 규격인 Canonical JSON(Canvas IR)으로 파싱한 후,
이를 DOCX와 HWPX로 동시 변환하는 엔진입니다.
동일한 Canonical JSON을 입력했을 때:
  - Microsoft Word (DOCX): 글자 겹침이 전혀 없이 완벽하게 렌더링됨.
  - 한컴오피스 2024 뷰어 (HWPX): 글자가 두 줄로 쪼개지며 직하단 요소를 침범하여 대규모 글자 겹침(Overlap) 발생.

Phase 20부터 Phase 23.1, 그리고 최근의 페이지 방향 교정까지 여러 차례의 수정이 진행되었으나,
사용자가 실제 한컴 2024 뷰어로 확인한 결과 텍스트 겹침은 여전히 해결되지 않고 있습니다.
따라서 현재 모든 렌더러 소스 코드 수정은 전면 동결(Freeze)되었으며,
추측에 의한 주먹구구식 수정을 영구 중단하고 근본적/아키텍처적 진단을 내리기 위해
본 마스터 Ingest 파일이 작성되었습니다.

본 Ingest에는 다음이 모두 무삭제 원문(Uncut Full Source)으로 포함되어 있습니다:
  1. 실제 충돌이 발생하는 Stage 7의 기하학적 실측 수치와 수학적 증명
  2. 생성된 HWPX / DOCX / 정품 한컴 레퍼런스 HWPX의 실제 XML 추출문
  3. 최소 2-요소 재현 단위(MRE) Canonical JSON
  4. 7개 핵심 소스 코드 및 3개 진단 스크립트 전문
  5. 7개 핵심 회귀 테스트 스위트 전문
  6. 8개 독립 감사 보고서 및 4개 방향 원인 분석 보고서 전문
  7. 감사관(Argon)이 답해야 할 10대 핵심 질문

================================================================================
SECTION 1: MATHEMATICAL & GEOMETRICAL PROOF OF COLLISION (Stage 7)
================================================================================

[충돌 메커니즘의 수학적 증명: Case Study - Page 1 '균열밀도 (CDI)' vs '0.031%']

1. 상위 텍스트박스 (ID: '7:980', '균열밀도 (CDI)'):
   - Canonical IR 좌표: x = 52.48 pt, y = 457.30 pt, width = 53.98 pt, height = 9.75 pt
   - 텍스트 속성: 글꼴 8.25 pt, 행간 11.13 pt, 글자 수 10자 ('균열밀도 (CDI)')
   - 글자 물리적 단일행 필요 폭:
     한글 4자(균열밀도) = 4 * 8.25 = 33.0 pt
     공백 1자 = 4.1 pt
     영문/기호 5자((CDI)) = 5 * 0.55 * 8.25 = 22.7 pt
     총 단일행 필요 폭 = 약 59.8 pt
   - 기하학적 모순: 박스 폭(53.98 pt) < 단일행 필요 폭(59.8 pt) -> [5.82 pt 결핍!]

2. 하위 텍스트박스 (ID: '7:981', '0.031%'):
   - Canonical IR 좌표: x = 52.48 pt, y = 473.06 pt, width = 55.48 pt, height = 17.99 pt

3. 두 요소 사이의 물리적 여백 (Vertical Gap):
   - 상위 박스 바닥 = 457.30 + 9.75 = 467.06 pt
   - 하위 박스 상단 = 473.06 pt
   - 물리적 여백 = 473.06 - 467.06 = 6.00 pt (극도로 좁음!)

4. 한컴 2024 뷰어에서의 파국 (lineWrap="BREAK" 적용 시):
   - 한컴 엔진은 박스 폭 53.98 pt에 맞추기 위해 '균열밀도'와 '(CDI)'로 강제 2줄 줄바꿈을 단행함.
   - 2줄 렌더링에 필요한 높이 = 11.13 * 2 = 22.26 pt.
   - 그러나 OWPML <hp:rect>의 높이는 9.75 pt (975 HWPU)로 고정되어 있음.
   - 2번째 줄 '(CDI)' 텍스트는 y = 457.30 + 11.13 = 468.43 pt에서 시작하여 476.68 pt까지 그려짐.
   - 476.68 pt > 473.06 pt 이므로, 정확히 3.62 pt 구간에 걸쳐 '0.031%' 숫자 위로 '(CDI)' 글자가 중첩 인쇄됨!

5. DOCX는 왜 충돌하지 않는가?
   - docxCanvasRenderer.ts는 isMultiLineBox를 통해 단일행 라벨에 mso-wrap-style:none을 강제함!
   - Word는 박스 폭이 53.98 pt여도 글자를 2줄로 쪼개지 않고 1줄로 유지함!
   - 따라서 높이가 9.75 pt로 유지되어 하단 0.031%와의 6.0 pt 여백이 온전히 지켜짐!

6. 정품 한컴 문서는 어떻게 해결했는가?
   - 정품 reference 문서(report-1차-진단-편집가능-최종보정.hwpx)는 lineWrap="SQUEEZE"를 지정함!
   - 한글 엔진이 장평/자간을 미세하게 압축하여 반드시 1줄로 강제 유지함!
   - 따라서 높이가 늘어나지 않아 하단 요소와 절대 충돌하지 않음!

SECTION 2: MINIMAL REPRODUCIBLE EXAMPLE (MRE) CANONICAL JSON
================================================================================
아래 JSON은 전체 복잡한 문서를 배제하고, 오직 충돌을 유발하는 2개 요소만을 추출한 최소 재현 데이터입니다:

{
  "title": "Minimal Reproduction: Single-line Label Vertical Collision",
  "pageSize": {
    "name": "A4",
    "widthPt": 595.28,
    "heightPt": 841.89,
    "orientation": "portrait",
    "unit": "pt"
  },
  "marginsPt": {
    "top": 0,
    "right": 0,
    "bottom": 0,
    "left": 0
  },
  "pages": [
    {
      "pageNumber": 1,
      "bounds": {
        "x": 0,
        "y": 0,
        "width": 595.28,
        "height": 841.89
      },
      "elements": [
        {
          "id": "label_cdi",
          "name": "균열밀도 (CDI)",
          "type": "text",
          "bounds": {
            "x": 52.48,
            "y": 457.3,
            "width": 53.98,
            "height": 9.75
          },
          "zIndex": 15,
          "textRuns": [
            {
              "text": "균열밀도 (CDI)",
              "fontSizePt": 8.25,
              "lineHeightPt": 11.13,
              "fontFamily": "맑은 고딕"
            }
          ]
        },
        {
          "id": "metric_val",
          "name": "0.031%",
          "type": "text",
          "bounds": {
            "x": 52.48,
            "y": 473.06,
            "width": 55.48,
            "height": 17.99
          },
          "zIndex": 16,
          "textRuns": [
            {
              "text": "0.031%",
              "fontSizePt": 14.99,
              "lineHeightPt": 20.24,
              "fontFamily": "맑은 고딕",
              "bold": true
            }
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.ts
================================================================================
import JSZip from "jszip";
import {
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasPage,
  CanvasElement,
  CanvasTextRun,
  CanvasResource,
  ptToHwpu,
} from "../types.js";
import { SvgPath } from "../geometry/svgPathParser.js";

export interface HwpxCanvasRendererOptions {
  creator?: string;
}

function escapeXml(str: string): string {
  if (!str) return "";
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&apos;")
    .replace(/[\x00-\x08\x0B\x0C\x0E-\x1F]/g, "");
}

function cleanHex(color?: string, fallback: string = "000000"): string {
  if (!color) return fallback;
  return color.replace("#", "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

interface CharPrKey {
  fontId: number;
  fontSizePt: number;
  colorHex: string;
  bold: boolean;
  italic: boolean;
  underline: boolean;
  strike: boolean;
  letterSpacingPct: number;
}

interface ParaPrEntry {
  id: number;
  horizontal: "LEFT" | "CENTER" | "RIGHT" | "JUSTIFY";
  spacingType: "PERCENT" | "FIXED";
  spacingValue: number;
  spacingUnit: "PERCENT" | "HWPUNIT";
}

export class HwpxCanvasRenderer {
  private creator: string;

  constructor(options?: HwpxCanvasRendererOptions) {
    this.creator = options?.creator || "Figma2Word Canvas Engine";
  }

  public async buildZip(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<JSZip> {
    const zip = new JSZip();

    // 1. mimetype (MUST be first entry, STORED uncompressed, 19 bytes)
    zip.file("mimetype", "application/hwp+zip", {
      compression: "STORE",
    });

    // 2. version.xml & settings.xml
    zip.file("version.xml", this.buildVersionXml());
    zip.file("settings.xml", this.buildSettingsXml());

    // 3. META-INF/container.xml
    zip.file("META-INF/container.xml", this.buildContainerXml());

    // 4. BinData/
    const imageBinMap = new Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>();
    let binCounter = 1;

    for (const [resId, res] of Object.entries(doc.resources || {})) {
      const binId = `BIN${binCounter.toString().padStart(4, "0")}`;
      const isJpeg =
        res.mimeType === "image/jpeg" ||
        res.mimeType === "image/jpg" ||
        (res.data && res.data.length >= 3 && res.data[0] === 0xff && res.data[1] === 0xd8);
      const ext = isJpeg ? "jpg" : "png";
      const mimeType = isJpeg ? "image/jpeg" : "image/png";
      const binFileName = `${binId}.${ext}`;
      imageBinMap.set(resId, { binId, ext, mimeType });
      zip.file(`BinData/${binFileName}`, res.data);
      binCounter++;
    }

    // 5. META-INF/manifest.xml & Contents/content.hpf
    zip.file("META-INF/manifest.xml", this.buildManifestXml(imageBinMap));
    zip.file("Contents/content.hpf", this.buildContentHpf(doc, imageBinMap));

    // 6. Dynamic Font Registry (7 OWPML languages)
    const fontRegistry = new Map<string, number>();
    fontRegistry.set("맑은 고딕", 0); // Default fallback at index 0

    for (const page of doc.pages) {
      for (const elem of page.elements) {
        if (elem.textRuns) {
          for (const run of elem.textRuns) {
            const font = run.fontFamily?.trim() || "맑은 고딕";
            if (!fontRegistry.has(font)) {
              fontRegistry.set(font, fontRegistry.size);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Dynamic style registries
    const charPrRegistry = new Map<string, number>(); // serializedKey -> id
    const charPrList: { id: number; key: CharPrKey }[] = [];

    const getCharPrId = (run: CanvasTextRun): number => {
      const font = run.fontFamily?.trim() || "맑은 고딕";
      const fontId = fontRegistry.get(font) ?? 0;
      const fontSizePt = Math.round((run.fontSizePt || 10) * 10) / 10;
      const letterSpacingPct =
        run.letterSpacingPt !== undefined && fontSizePt > 0
          ? Math.max(-50, Math.min(50, Math.round((run.letterSpacingPt / fontSizePt) * 100)))
          : 0;
      const key: CharPrKey = {
        fontId,
        fontSizePt,
        colorHex: cleanHex(run.color, "000000"),
        bold: (run.fontWeight || 400) >= 600,
        italic: !!run.italic,
        underline: !!run.underline,
        strike: !!run.strike,
        letterSpacingPct,
      };
      const serialized = `${fontId}_${key.fontSizePt}_${key.colorHex}_${key.bold}_${key.italic}_${key.underline}_${key.strike}_${key.letterSpacingPct}`;
      if (charPrRegistry.has(serialized)) {
        return charPrRegistry.get(serialized)!;
      }
      const newId = charPrList.length;
      charPrRegistry.set(serialized, newId);
      charPrList.push({ id: newId, key });
      return newId;
    };

    const paraPrList: ParaPrEntry[] = [
      { id: 0, horizontal: "LEFT", spacingType: "PERCENT", spacingValue: 160, spacingUnit: "PERCENT" },
      { id: 1, horizontal: "CENTER", spacingType: "PERCENT", spacingValue: 160, spacingUnit: "PERCENT" },
      { id: 2, horizontal: "RIGHT", spacingType: "PERCENT", spacingValue: 160, spacingUnit: "PERCENT" },
      { id: 3, horizontal: "JUSTIFY", spacingType: "PERCENT", spacingValue: 160, spacingUnit: "PERCENT" },
    ];
    const paraPrRegistry = new Map<string, number>([
      ["LEFT_PERCENT_160", 0],
      ["CENTER_PERCENT_160", 1],
      ["RIGHT_PERCENT_160", 2],
      ["JUSTIFY_PERCENT_160", 3],
    ]);

    const getParaPrId = (
      align?: "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify",
      lineHeightPt?: number
    ): number => {
      const horizontal: "LEFT" | "CENTER" | "RIGHT" | "JUSTIFY" =
        align === "center"
          ? "CENTER"
          : align === "right"
          ? "RIGHT"
          : align === "justify"
          ? "JUSTIFY"
          : "LEFT";
      if (!lineHeightPt || lineHeightPt <= 0) {
        return paraPrRegistry.get(`${horizontal}_PERCENT_160`) ?? 0;
      }
      const spacingValue = Math.max(100, Math.round(lineHeightPt * 100));
      const key = `${horizontal}_FIXED_${spacingValue}`;
      if (paraPrRegistry.has(key)) {
        return paraPrRegistry.get(key)!;
      }
      const newId = paraPrList.length;
      paraPrRegistry.set(key, newId);
      paraPrList.push({
        id: newId,
        horizontal,
        spacingType: "FIXED",
        spacingValue,
        spacingUnit: "HWPUNIT",
      });
      return newId;
    };

    // 7. Contents/section0.xml (builds shapes & populates charPrRegistry / paraPrRegistry)
    const section0Xml = this.buildSection0Xml(doc, imageBinMap, getCharPrId, getParaPrId);
    zip.file("Contents/section0.xml", section0Xml);

    // 8. Contents/header.xml (outputs registered fontfaces, charPr & paraPr styles)
    zip.file("Contents/header.xml", this.buildHeaderXml(charPrList, fontRegistry, paraPrList, 1));

    return zip;
  }

  public async renderToUint8Array(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    const zip = await this.buildZip(doc);
    return await zip.generateAsync({
      type: "uint8array",
      compression: "DEFLATE",
      compressionOptions: { level: 6 },
    });
  }

  public async renderToBlob(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Blob> {
    const uint8 = await this.renderToUint8Array(doc);
    return new Blob([uint8 as any], {
      type: "application/hwp+zip",
    });
  }

  public async renderToBuffer(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Buffer> {
    const uint8 = await this.renderToUint8Array(doc);
    return Buffer.from(uint8.buffer, uint8.byteOffset, uint8.byteLength);
  }

  private buildVersionXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<hv:HCFVersion xmlns:hv="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/version" tagetApplication="WORDPROCESSOR" major="5" minor="1" micro="0" buildNumber="1" os="1" xmlVersion="1.2" application="Hancom Office Hangul" appVersion="10, 0, 0, 7888 WIN32LEWindows_8"/>`.trim();
  }

  private buildSettingsXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<ha:HWPApplicationSetting xmlns:ha="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/app">
  <ha:CaretPosition listIDRef="0" paraIDRef="0" pos="0"/>
</ha:HWPApplicationSetting>`.trim();
  }

  private buildContainerXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<container xmlns="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:container" version="1.0">
  <rootfiles>
    <rootfile full-path="Contents/content.hpf" media-type="application/hwpml-package+xml"/>
  </rootfiles>
</container>`.trim();
  }

  private buildManifestXml(
    imageBinMap: Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>
  ): string {
    let items = `  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="/" manifest:media-type="application/hwp+zip"/>
  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="Contents/content.hpf" manifest:media-type="application/hwpml-package+xml"/>
  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="Contents/header.xml" manifest:media-type="application/xml"/>
  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="Contents/section0.xml" manifest:media-type="application/xml"/>
  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="settings.xml" manifest:media-type="application/xml"/>`;

    for (const { binId, ext, mimeType } of imageBinMap.values()) {
      items += `\n  <manifest:file-entry manifest:full-path="BinData/${binId}.${ext}" manifest:media-type="${mimeType}"/>`;
    }

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<manifest:manifest xmlns:manifest="urn:oasis:names:tc:opendocument:xmlns:manifest:1.0">
${items}
</manifest:manifest>`.trim();
  }

  private buildContentHpf(
    doc: CanvasDocument,
    imageBinMap: Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>
  ): string {
    let binItems = "";
    for (const { binId, ext, mimeType } of imageBinMap.values()) {
      binItems += `\n    <opf:item id="${binId}" href="BinData/${binId}.${ext}" media-type="${mimeType}" isEmbeded="1"/>`;
    }

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<opf:package xmlns:opf="http://www.idpf.org/2007/opf/" version="1.2" unique-identifier="bookid">
  <opf:metadata>
    <opf:title>${escapeXml(doc.title)}</opf:title>
    <opf:language>ko</opf:language>
  </opf:metadata>
  <opf:manifest>
    <opf:item id="header" href="Contents/header.xml" media-type="application/xml"/>
    <opf:item id="section0" href="Contents/section0.xml" media-type="application/xml"/>${binItems}
  </opf:manifest>
  <opf:spine>
    <opf:itemref idref="header"/>
    <opf:itemref idref="section0"/>
  </opf:spine>
</opf:package>`.trim();
  }

  private buildHeaderXml(
    charPrList: { id: number; key: CharPrKey }[],
    fontRegistry: Map<string, number>,
    paraPrList: ParaPrEntry[],
    secCnt: number = 1
  ): string {
    let charPrEntries = "";
    for (const { id, key } of charPrList) {
      const heightHwpu = Math.round(key.fontSizePt * 100);
      const boldAttr = key.bold ? ' bold="1"' : "";
      const italicAttr = key.italic ? ' italic="1"' : "";
      const ls = key.letterSpacingPct || 0;
      const underlineXml = key.underline
        ? `\n      <hh:underline type="BOTTOM" shape="SOLID" color="#${key.colorHex}"/>`
        : "";
      const strikeXml = key.strike
        ? `\n      <hh:strikeout shape="CONTINUOUS" color="#${key.colorHex}"/>`
        : "";
      charPrEntries += `\n    <hh:charPr id="${id}" height="${heightHwpu}" textColor="#${key.colorHex}" shadeColor="none" useFontSpace="1" useKerning="1" symMark="NONE" borderFillIDRef="1"${boldAttr}${italicAttr}>
      <hh:fontRef hangul="${key.fontId}" latin="${key.fontId}" hanja="${key.fontId}" japanese="${key.fontId}" other="${key.fontId}" symbol="${key.fontId}" user="${key.fontId}"/>
      <hh:ratio hangul="100" latin="100" hanja="100" japanese="100" other="100" symbol="100" user="100"/>
      <hh:spacing hangul="${ls}" latin="${ls}" hanja="${ls}" japanese="${ls}" other="${ls}" symbol="${ls}" user="${ls}"/>
      <hh:relSz hangul="100" latin="100" hanja="100" japanese="100" other="100" symbol="100" user="100"/>
      <hh:offset hangul="0" latin="0" hanja="0" japanese="0" other="0" symbol="0" user="0"/>${underlineXml}${strikeXml}
    </hh:charPr>`;
    }

    if (charPrList.length === 0) {
      charPrEntries = `\n    <hh:charPr id="0" height="1000" textColor="#000000" shadeColor="none" useFontSpace="1" useKerning="1" symMark="NONE" borderFillIDRef="1">
      <hh:fontRef hangul="0" latin="0" hanja="0" japanese="0" other="0" symbol="0" user="0"/>
      <hh:ratio hangul="100" latin="100" hanja="100" japanese="100" other="100" symbol="100" user="100"/>
      <hh:spacing hangul="0" latin="0" hanja="0" japanese="0" other="0" symbol="0" user="0"/>
      <hh:relSz hangul="100" latin="100" hanja="100" japanese="100" other="100" symbol="100" user="100"/>
      <hh:offset hangul="0" latin="0" hanja="0" japanese="0" other="0" symbol="0" user="0"/>
      <hh:underline type="NONE" shape="SOLID" color="#000000"/>
      <hh:strikeout shape="NONE" color="#000000"/>
    </hh:charPr>`;
    }

    const paraPrEntries = paraPrList
      .map(
        (p) => `      <hh:paraPr id="${p.id}" tabPrIDRef="0" condense="0" fontLineHeight="0" snapToGrid="0" suppressLineNumbers="0" checked="0">
        <hh:align horizontal="${p.horizontal}" vertical="BASELINE"/>
        <hh:heading type="NONE" idRef="0" level="0"/>
        <hh:breakSetting breakLatinWord="KEEP_WORD" breakNonLatinWord="BREAK_WORD" widowOrphan="0" keepWithNext="0" keepLines="0" pageBreakBefore="0" lineWrap="BREAK"/>
        <hh:autoSpacing eAsianEng="0" eAsianNum="0"/>
        <hh:margin><hc:intent value="0" unit="HWPUNIT"/><hc:left value="0" unit="HWPUNIT"/><hc:right value="0" unit="HWPUNIT"/><hc:prev value="0" unit="HWPUNIT"/><hc:next value="0" unit="HWPUNIT"/></hh:margin>
        <hh:lineSpacing type="${p.spacingType}" value="${p.spacingValue}" unit="${p.spacingUnit}"/>
        <hh:border borderFillIDRef="1" offsetLeft="0" offsetRight="0" offsetTop="0" offsetBottom="0" connect="0" ignoreMargin="0"/>
      </hh:paraPr>`
      )
      .join("\n");

    const langs = ["HANGUL", "LATIN", "HANJA", "JAPANESE", "OTHER", "SYMBOL", "USER"];
    const fontCnt = fontRegistry.size;
    const fontXmlEntries = Array.from(fontRegistry.entries())
      .map(([face, id]) => `<hh:font id="${id}" face="${escapeXml(face)}" type="TTF" isEmbedded="0"/>`)
      .join("\n        ");

    const fontfaceBlocks = langs
      .map(
        (lang) => `<hh:fontface lang="${lang}" fontCnt="${fontCnt}">
        ${fontXmlEntries}
      </hh:fontface>`
      )
      .join("\n      ");

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<hh:head xmlns:hh="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/head" xmlns:hc="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/core" version="1.2" secCnt="${secCnt}">
  <hh:beginNum page="1" footnote="1" endnote="1" pic="1" tbl="1" equation="1"/>
  <hh:refList>
    <hh:fontfaces itemCnt="7">
      ${fontfaceBlocks}
    </hh:fontfaces>
    <hh:borderFills itemCnt="1">
      <hh:borderFill id="1" threeD="0" shadow="0" centerLine="NONE" breakCellSeparateLine="0">
        <hh:slash type="NONE" Crooked="0" isCounter="0"/>
        <hh:backSlash type="NONE" Crooked="0" isCounter="0"/>
        <hh:leftBorder type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hh:rightBorder type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hh:topBorder type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hh:bottomBorder type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hh:diagonal type="NONE" width="0.1 mm" color="#000000"/>
        <hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="none" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>
      </hh:borderFill>
    </hh:borderFills>
    <hh:charProperties itemCnt="${Math.max(1, charPrList.length)}">${charPrEntries}
    </hh:charProperties>
    <hh:paraProperties itemCnt="${paraPrList.length}">
${paraPrEntries}
    </hh:paraProperties>
    <hh:styles itemCnt="1">
      <hh:style id="0" type="PARA" name="바탕글" engName="Normal" paraPrIDRef="0" charPrIDRef="0" nextStyleIDRef="0"/>
    </hh:styles>
  </hh:refList>
</hh:head>`.trim();
  }

  private buildSection0Xml(
    doc: CanvasDocument,
    imageBinMap: Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>,
    getCharPrId: (run: CanvasTextRun) => number,
    getParaPrId: (align?: "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify", lineHeightPt?: number) => number
  ): string {
    const pageParas: string[] = [];
    let globalShapeId = 1;
    const nextId = () => globalShapeId++;

    for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < doc.pages.length; pIdx++) {
      const page = doc.pages[pIdx];
      const pageBreakAttr = pIdx === 0 ? ' pageBreak="0"' : ' pageBreak="1"';

      // Dynamic page size and orientation (per page)
      const pageWidthPt = page.bounds.width || doc.pageSize.widthPt || 595.28;
      const pageHeightPt = page.bounds.height || doc.pageSize.heightPt || 841.89;
      const isLandscape = pageWidthPt > pageHeightPt;
      // In KS X 6101 (OWPML): WIDELY = Portrait (세로), NARROWLY = Landscape (가로)
      const landscapeAttr = isLandscape ? "NARROWLY" : "WIDELY";
      // OWPML paper dimensions are strictly defined in portrait-base coordinates (width <= height)
      const baseWidthPt = isLandscape ? Math.min(pageWidthPt, pageHeightPt) : pageWidthPt;
      const baseHeightPt = isLandscape ? Math.max(pageWidthPt, pageHeightPt) : pageHeightPt;
      const widthHwpu = Math.round(baseWidthPt * 100);
      const heightHwpu = Math.round(baseHeightPt * 100);

      const shapesXml: string[] = [];

      // 1. Full-page background rect if specified
      if (page.backgroundColor) {
        const bgW = ptToHwpu(pageWidthPt);
        const bgH = ptToHwpu(pageHeightPt);
        const bgId = nextId();
        const bgRect = this.renderRectXml({
          id: bgId,
          zOrder: 0,
          x: 0,
          y: 0,
          w: bgW,
          h: bgH,
          fillColorHex: cleanHex(page.backgroundColor),
          lineColorHex: "000000",
          lineWidth: "0",
          lineStyle: "NONE",
          ratio: 0,
        });
        shapesXml.push(`<hp:run>${bgRect}</hp:run>`);
      }

      // 2. Render all elements
      for (const elem of page.elements) {
        const rendered = this.renderElement(
          nextId,
          elem,
          imageBinMap,
          getCharPrId,
          getParaPrId,
          doc.resources
        );
        for (const shapeXml of rendered) {
          shapesXml.push(`<hp:run>${shapeXml}</hp:run>`);
        }
      }

      // Section properties in hp:run inside the first hp:p (or on page orientation/dimension change)
      const prevPage = pIdx > 0 ? doc.pages[pIdx - 1] : undefined;
      const prevWidthPt = prevPage ? (prevPage.bounds.width || doc.pageSize.widthPt) : undefined;
      const prevHeightPt = prevPage ? (prevPage.bounds.height || doc.pageSize.heightPt) : undefined;
      const orientationChanged =
        prevWidthPt !== undefined &&
        (Math.abs(prevWidthPt - pageWidthPt) > 1 || Math.abs(prevHeightPt! - pageHeightPt) > 1);

      const secPrRunXml =
        pIdx === 0 || orientationChanged
          ? `<hp:run charPrIDRef="0">
      <hp:secPr id="" textDirection="HORIZONTAL" spaceColumns="1134" tabStop="8000" tabStopVal="4000" tabStopUnit="HWPUNIT" outlineShapeIDRef="1" memoShapeIDRef="0" textVerticalWidthHead="0" masterPageCnt="0">
        <hp:grid lineGrid="0" charGrid="0" wonggojiFormat="0"/>
        <hp:startNum pageStartsOn="BOTH" page="${pIdx + 1}" pic="1" tbl="1" equation="1"/>
        <hp:visibility hideFirstHeader="0" hideFirstFooter="0" hideFirstMasterPage="0" border="NONE" fill="NONE" hideFirstPageNum="0" hideFirstEmptyLine="0" showLineNumber="0"/>
        <hp:lineNumberShape restartType="0" countBy="0" distance="0" startNumber="0"/>
        <hp:pagePr landscape="${landscapeAttr}" width="${widthHwpu}" height="${heightHwpu}" gutterType="LEFT_ONLY">
          <hp:margin header="0" footer="0" gutter="0" left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
        </hp:pagePr>
        <hp:footNotePr>
          <hp:autoNumFormat type="DIGIT" userChar="" prefixChar="" suffixChar=")" supscript="0"/>
          <hp:noteLine length="-1" type="SOLID" width="0.12 mm" color="#000000"/>
          <hp:noteSpacing betweenNotes="283" belowLine="567" aboveLine="850"/>
          <hp:numbering type="CONTINUOUS" newNum="1"/>
          <hp:placement place="EACH_COLUMN" beneathText="0"/>
        </hp:footNotePr>
        <hp:endNotePr>
          <hp:autoNumFormat type="DIGIT" userChar="" prefixChar="" suffixChar=")" supscript="0"/>
          <hp:noteLine length="-1" type="SOLID" width="0.12 mm" color="#000000"/>
          <hp:noteSpacing betweenNotes="0" belowLine="567" aboveLine="850"/>
          <hp:numbering type="CONTINUOUS" newNum="1"/>
          <hp:placement place="END_OF_DOCUMENT" beneathText="0"/>
        </hp:endNotePr>
        <hp:pageBorderFill type="BOTH" borderFillIDRef="1" textBorder="PAPER" headerInside="0" footerInside="0" fillArea="PAPER">
          <hp:offset left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
        </hp:pageBorderFill>
      </hp:secPr>
      <hp:ctrl>
        <hp:colPr id="" type="NEWSPAPER" layout="LEFT" colCnt="1" sameSz="1" sameGap="0"/>
      </hp:ctrl>
    </hp:run>`
          : "";

      const allRuns = [secPrRunXml, ...shapesXml].filter(Boolean);
      pageParas.push(`<hp:p id="${pIdx}" paraPrIDRef="0" styleIDRef="0"${pageBreakAttr} columnBreak="0" merged="0">
  ${allRuns.join("\n  ")}
</hp:p>`);
    }

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<hs:sec xmlns:hs="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/section"
        xmlns:hp="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/paragraph"
        xmlns:hc="http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2011/core">
  ${pageParas.join("\n  ")}
</hs:sec>`.trim();
  }

  private renderElement(
    nextId: () => number,
    elem: CanvasElement,
    imageBinMap: Map<string, { binId: string; ext: string; mimeType: string }>,
    getCharPrId: (run: CanvasTextRun) => number,
    getParaPrId: (align?: "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify", lineHeightPt?: number) => number,
    resources?: Record<string, CanvasResource>
  ): string[] {
    const xHwpu = ptToHwpu(elem.bounds.x);
    const yHwpu = ptToHwpu(elem.bounds.y);
    const wHwpu = Math.max(100, ptToHwpu(elem.bounds.width));
    const hHwpu = Math.max(100, ptToHwpu(elem.bounds.height));
    const zOrder = elem.zIndex;

    // 1. Image with FILL Cover Crop, Explicit CROP, or FIT Letterbox
    if (elem.type === "image" && elem.imageRef) {
      const binEntry = imageBinMap.get(elem.imageRef);
      if (binEntry) {
        const binId = binEntry.binId;
        const res = resources?.[elem.imageRef];
        let imgW = 0;
        let imgH = 0;

        if (res && res.data) {
          const d = res.data;
          const readU16 = (off: number) => ((d[off] || 0) << 8) | (d[off + 1] || 0);
          const readU32 = (off: number) =>
            (((d[off] || 0) << 24) >>> 0) +
            ((d[off + 1] || 0) << 16) +
            ((d[off + 2] || 0) << 8) +
            (d[off + 3] || 0);

          if (d.length >= 24 && d[0] === 0x89 && d[1] === 0x50 && d[2] === 0x4e && d[3] === 0x47) {
            imgW = readU32(16);
            imgH = readU32(20);
          } else if (d.length >= 4 && d[0] === 0xff && d[1] === 0xd8) {
            let offset = 2;
            while (offset < d.length - 8) {
              const marker = readU16(offset);
              offset += 2;
              if (marker === 0xffc0 || marker === 0xffc2) {
                imgH = readU16(offset + 3);
                imgW = readU16(offset + 5);
                break;
              }
              const len = readU16(offset);
              offset += len;
            }
          }
        }

        let picX = xHwpu;
        let picY = yHwpu;
        let picW = wHwpu;
        let picH = hHwpu;
        let dimW = imgW > 0 ? Math.round(imgW * 75) : wHwpu;
        let dimH = imgH > 0 ? Math.round(imgH * 75) : hHwpu;
        let clipLeft = 0;
        let clipRight = dimW;
        let clipTop = 0;
        let clipBottom = dimH;

        if (
          elem.imageCrop &&
          (elem.imageCrop.top > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.bottom > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.left > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.right > 0)
        ) {
          const cLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.left));
          const cRight = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.right));
          const cTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.top));
          const cBottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.bottom));
          clipLeft = Math.round(dimW * cLeft);
          clipRight = Math.round(dimW * (1 - cRight));
          clipTop = Math.round(dimH * cTop);
          clipBottom = Math.round(dimH * (1 - cBottom));
        } else if (elem.imageScaleMode === "FIT" && imgW > 0 && imgH > 0) {
          const arImg = imgW / imgH;
          const arBox = wHwpu / hHwpu;
          if (arImg > arBox * 1.01) {
            picH = Math.max(100, Math.round(wHwpu / arImg));
            picY = Math.round(yHwpu + (hHwpu - picH) / 2);
          } else if (arImg < arBox * 0.99) {
            picW = Math.max(100, Math.round(hHwpu * arImg));
            picX = Math.round(xHwpu + (wHwpu - picW) / 2);
          }
          clipLeft = 0;
          clipRight = dimW;
          clipTop = 0;
          clipBottom = dimH;
        } else if (imgW > 0 && imgH > 0) {
          const arImg = imgW / imgH;
          const arBox = wHwpu / hHwpu;

          if (arImg > arBox * 1.01) {
            const visW = dimH * arBox;
            const cropX = (dimW - visW) / 2.0;
            clipLeft = Math.round(cropX);
            clipRight = Math.round(dimW - cropX);
            clipTop = 0;
            clipBottom = dimH;
          } else if (arImg < arBox * 0.99) {
            const visH = dimW / arBox;
            const cropY = (dimH - visH) / 2.0;
            clipLeft = 0;
            clipRight = dimW;
            clipTop = Math.round(cropY);
            clipBottom = Math.round(dimH - cropY);
          } else {
            clipLeft = 0;
            clipRight = dimW;
            clipTop = 0;
            clipBottom = dimH;
          }
        }

        const picId = nextId();
        return [
          this.renderPicXml({
            id: picId,
            zOrder,
            x: picX,
            y: picY,
            w: picW,
            h: picH,
            binId,
            clipLeft,
            clipRight,
            clipTop,
            clipBottom,
            dimW,
            dimH,
          }),
        ];
      }
    }

    // 2. Line (Canonical representation: thin filled rect for H/V dividers matching authentic Hancom, or native hp:line)
    if (elem.type === "line") {
      const isHorizontal = elem.bounds.height <= 4 || elem.bounds.width >= elem.bounds.height * 2;
      const isVertical = elem.bounds.width <= 4 || elem.bounds.height >= elem.bounds.width * 2;
      const isSolid = !elem.style.strokeDashed;

      // Authentic Hancom standard: 100% of horizontal/vertical dividers are serialized as thin filled rectangles
      if ((isHorizontal || isVertical) && isSolid) {
        const rectId = nextId();
        const strokeColor = cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor, "000000");
        const strokeWidthPt = elem.style.strokeWidth || 1;
        const strokeHwpu = Math.max(75, Math.round(strokeWidthPt * 100));

        const lineW = isHorizontal ? wHwpu : strokeHwpu;
        const lineH = isHorizontal ? strokeHwpu : hHwpu;

        return [
          this.renderRectXml({
            id: rectId,
            zOrder,
            x: xHwpu,
            y: yHwpu,
            w: lineW,
            h: lineH,
            fillColorHex: strokeColor,
            lineColorHex: "000000",
            lineWidth: "0",
            lineStyle: "NONE",
            ratio: 0,
          }),
        ];
      }

      // Otherwise render as native KS X 6101 / OWPML <hp:line> with valid startPt/endPt and isReverseHV="0"
      const lineId = nextId();
      const strokeColor = cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor, "000000");
      const strokeW = Math.max(10, Math.round((elem.style.strokeWidth || 1) * 10)).toString();
      const strokeStyle = elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID";
      const lineShapeW = Math.max(1, wHwpu);
      const lineShapeH = Math.max(0, hHwpu);
      return [
        this.renderLineXml({
          id: lineId,
          zOrder,
          x: xHwpu,
          y: yHwpu,
          w: lineShapeW,
          h: lineShapeH,
          startPt: { x: 0, y: 0 },
          endPt: { x: lineShapeW, y: lineShapeH },
          colorHex: strokeColor,
          width: strokeW,
          style: strokeStyle,
          endCap: "FLAT",
        }),
      ];
    }

    // Common text & style calculation
    const isText = elem.type === "text" || (elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0);
    const hasFill = !!elem.style.fillColor;
    const hasStroke = !!elem.style.strokeColor && (elem.style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

    const lineStyle = hasStroke ? (elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID") : "NONE";
    const lineColor = hasStroke ? cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor, "000000") : "000000";
    const lineWidth = hasStroke ? Math.round((elem.style.strokeWidth || 1) * 10).toString() : "0";

    const fillBrushXml = hasFill
      ? `<hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor)}" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>`
      : `<hc:fillBrush/>`;

    const shapeW = wHwpu;
    const shapeH = hHwpu;

    let drawTextPart = "";
    if (isText && elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0) {
      const firstLineHeightPt = elem.textRuns.find((r) => r.lineHeightPt && r.lineHeightPt > 0)?.lineHeightPt;
      const paraPrId = getParaPrId(elem.paragraphAlign, firstLineHeightPt);

      // Split runs across lines into separate paragraphs
      const paragraphs: Array<Array<{ charPrId: number; text: string }>> = [[]];

      for (const run of elem.textRuns) {
        const charPrId = getCharPrId(run);
        const lines = run.text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
          if (i > 0) {
            paragraphs.push([]);
          }
          if (lines[i].length > 0 || lines.length === 1) {
            paragraphs[paragraphs.length - 1].push({ charPrId, text: lines[i] });
          }
        }
      }

      const parasXml: string[] = paragraphs.map((paraRuns, pIdx) => {
        const runsStr =
          paraRuns.length > 0
            ? paraRuns
                .map((r) => `<hp:run charPrIDRef="${r.charPrId}"><hp:t>${escapeXml(r.text)}</hp:t></hp:run>`)
                .join("")
            : `<hp:run charPrIDRef="0"><hp:t/></hp:run>`;
        return `<hp:p id="${pIdx}" paraPrIDRef="${paraPrId}" styleIDRef="0" pageBreak="0" columnBreak="0" merged="0">${runsStr}</hp:p>`;
      });

      const drawName = escapeXml(elem.name || elem.id || "");
      const cornerRadiusPt = elem.style?.cornerRadius || 0;
      const isPillOrBadge =
        (elem.bounds.height <= 36 || cornerRadiusPt >= elem.bounds.height / 3) && paragraphs.length <= 1;

      const vertAlignAttr =
        elem.verticalAlign === "center" || (hasFill && !elem.verticalAlign && isPillOrBadge)
          ? "CENTER"
          : elem.verticalAlign === "bottom"
          ? "BOTTOM"
          : "TOP";

      // Margin in OWPML textMargin: strictly use explicit innerPaddingPt, otherwise 0 matching authentic Hancom standard
      const marginL = elem.innerPaddingPt?.left ? ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.left) : 0;
      const marginR = elem.innerPaddingPt?.right ? ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.right) : 0;
      const marginT = elem.innerPaddingPt?.top ? ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.top) : 0;
      const marginB = elem.innerPaddingPt?.bottom ? ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.bottom) : 0;

      drawTextPart = `
  <hp:drawText lastWidth="${shapeW}" name="${drawName}" editable="1">
    <hp:subList id="" textDirection="HORIZONTAL" lineWrap="BREAK" vertAlign="${vertAlignAttr}" linkListIDRef="0" linkListNextIDRef="0" textWidth="${shapeW}" textHeight="0" hasTextRef="0" hasNumRef="0">
      ${parasXml.join("\n      ")}
    </hp:subList>
    <hp:textMargin left="${marginL}" right="${marginR}" top="${marginT}" bottom="${marginB}"/>
  </hp:drawText>`;
    }

    // 3. Vector Shape (KS X 6101 native hp:polygon for closed paths / hp:line for open subpaths)
    if (elem.type === "vector") {
      let subpaths = elem.vectorSubpaths;
      if (!subpaths || subpaths.length === 0) {
        if (elem.rawSvgPath) {
          subpaths = SvgPath.parse(elem.rawSvgPath).toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(shapeW, shapeH);
        } else if (elem.vectorPoints && elem.vectorPoints.length > 0) {
          subpaths = [{ points: elem.vectorPoints, closed: true }];
        }
      }

      if (subpaths && subpaths.length > 0) {
        const renderedShapes: string[] = [];
        for (let idx = 0; idx < subpaths.length; idx++) {
          const sp = subpaths[idx];
          const pts = sp.points;

          // Open subpath: emit connected <hp:line> segments
          if (!sp.closed && pts.length >= 2) {
            const openStrokeColor = hasStroke
              ? lineColor
              : hasFill
              ? cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor, "000000")
              : "000000";
            const openStrokeWidth = hasStroke ? lineWidth : "15";
            const openStrokeStyle = elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID";
            const endCap = pts.length > 2 ? "ROUND" : "FLAT";

            for (let segIdx = 0; segIdx < pts.length - 1; segIdx++) {
              const p0 = pts[segIdx];
              const p1 = pts[segIdx + 1];
              const lineId = nextId();
              renderedShapes.push(
                this.renderLineXml({
                  id: lineId,
                  zOrder,
                  x: xHwpu,
                  y: yHwpu,
                  w: shapeW,
                  h: shapeH,
                  p0: { x: p0.x, y: p0.y },
                  p1: { x: p1.x, y: p1.y },
                  colorHex: openStrokeColor,
                  width: openStrokeWidth,
                  style: openStrokeStyle,
                  endCap,
                })
              );
            }
            continue;
          }

          // Closed polygon
          const polyId = nextId();
          const subFillBrush =
            sp.closed && hasFill
              ? fillBrushXml
              : `<hc:fillBrush/>`;

          const polyPts =
            pts.length > 0
              ? pts
              : [
                  { x: 0, y: 0 },
                  { x: shapeW, y: 0 },
                  { x: shapeW, y: shapeH },
                  { x: 0, y: shapeH },
                ];

          renderedShapes.push(
            this.renderPolygonXml({
              id: polyId,
              zOrder,
              x: xHwpu,
              y: yHwpu,
              w: shapeW,
              h: shapeH,
              points: polyPts,
              colorHex: lineColor,
              width: lineWidth,
              style: lineStyle,
              fillBrushXml: subFillBrush,
              drawTextXml: idx === 0 ? drawTextPart : "",
            })
          );
        }

        return renderedShapes;
      }
    }

    // 4. Oval / Ellipse
    if (elem.type === "oval") {
      const ovalId = nextId();
      return [
        this.renderOvalXml({
          id: ovalId,
          zOrder,
          x: xHwpu,
          y: yHwpu,
          w: shapeW,
          h: shapeH,
          colorHex: lineColor,
          width: lineWidth,
          style: lineStyle,
          fillBrushXml,
          drawTextXml: drawTextPart,
        }),
      ];
    }

    // 5. Card / Rect / Textbox
    const cornerRadius = elem.style?.cornerRadius || 0;
    const minDim = Math.min(elem.bounds.width, elem.bounds.height);
    const ratio =
      cornerRadius > 0 && minDim > 0
        ? Math.min(50, Math.max(0, Math.round((cornerRadius / (minDim / 2)) * 50)))
        : 0;

    const rectId = nextId();
    return [
      this.renderRectXml({
        id: rectId,
        zOrder,
        x: xHwpu,
        y: yHwpu,
        w: shapeW,
        h: shapeH,
        lineColorHex: lineColor,
        lineWidth: lineWidth,
        lineStyle: lineStyle,
        colorHex: lineColor,
        width: lineWidth,
        style: lineStyle,
        fillBrushXml,
        drawTextXml: drawTextPart,
        ratio,
      }),
    ];
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:rect> with strict KS X 6101 element sequence and coordinates
   */
  private renderRectXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    fillColorHex?: string;
    lineColorHex?: string;
    lineWidth?: string;
    lineStyle?: string;
    colorHex?: string;
    width?: string;
    style?: string;
    ratio: number;
    fillBrushXml?: string;
    drawTextXml?: string;
  }): string {
    const rawColor = p.lineColorHex || p.colorHex || "000000";
    const rawWidth = p.lineWidth || p.width || "0";
    const rawStyle = p.lineStyle || p.style || "NONE";

    const cleanLineColor = cleanHex(rawColor, "000000");
    const safeWidth = /^\d+$/.test(String(rawWidth).trim()) ? String(rawWidth).trim() : "0";
    const safeStyle = ["NONE", "SOLID", "DASH", "DOT", "DASH_DOT", "DASH_DOT_DOT"].includes(rawStyle)
      ? rawStyle
      : "NONE";

    const fillBrush =
      p.fillBrushXml ||
      (p.fillColorHex
        ? `<hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="#${cleanHex(p.fillColorHex)}" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>`
        : `<hc:fillBrush/>`);

    const drawText = p.drawTextXml || "";

    return `<hp:rect id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}" ratio="${p.ratio}">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(p.w / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(p.h / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${cleanLineColor}" width="${safeWidth}" style="${safeStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  ${fillBrush}${drawText}
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  <hc:pt0 x="0" y="0"/>
  <hc:pt1 x="${p.w}" y="0"/>
  <hc:pt2 x="${p.w}" y="${p.h}"/>
  <hc:pt3 x="0" y="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:rect>`;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:ellipse> conforming to KS X 6101 and authentic Hancom documents
   */
  private renderOvalXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    colorHex: string;
    width: string;
    style: string;
    fillBrushXml: string;
    drawTextXml: string;
  }): string {
    const cx = Math.round(p.w / 2);
    const cy = Math.round(p.h / 2);

    const safeColor = cleanHex(p.colorHex, "000000");
    const safeWidth = /^\d+$/.test(String(p.width).trim()) ? String(p.width).trim() : "0";
    const safeStyle = ["NONE", "SOLID", "DASH", "DOT", "DASH_DOT", "DASH_DOT_DOT"].includes(p.style)
      ? p.style
      : "NONE";

    return `<hp:ellipse intervalDirty="0" hasArcPr="0" arcType="NORMAL" id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${cx}" centerY="${cy}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${safeColor}" width="${safeWidth}" style="${safeStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  ${p.fillBrushXml}${p.drawTextXml}
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  <hc:center x="${cx}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:ax1 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:ax2 x="${cx}" y="${p.h}"/>
  <hc:start1 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:end1 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:start2 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:end2 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:ellipse>`;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:line> with hc:startPt, hc:endPt conforming to OWPML LineType
   */
  private renderLineXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    startPt?: { x: number; y: number };
    endPt?: { x: number; y: number };
    p0?: { x: number; y: number };
    p1?: { x: number; y: number };
    colorHex: string;
    width: string;
    style: string;
    endCap: string;
  }): string {
    const safeColor = cleanHex(p.colorHex, "000000");
    const safeWidth = /^\d+$/.test(String(p.width).trim()) ? String(p.width).trim() : "10";
    const safeStyle = ["NONE", "SOLID", "DASH", "DOT", "DASH_DOT", "DASH_DOT_DOT"].includes(p.style)
      ? p.style
      : "SOLID";

    const startX = p.startPt?.x ?? p.p0?.x ?? 0;
    const startY = p.startPt?.y ?? p.p0?.y ?? 0;
    const endX = p.endPt?.x ?? p.p1?.x ?? p.w;
    const endY = p.endPt?.y ?? p.p1?.y ?? p.h;

    return `<hp:line id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}" isReverseHV="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(p.w / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(p.h / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${safeColor}" width="${safeWidth}" style="${safeStyle}" endCap="${p.endCap}" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  <hc:startPt x="${startX}" y="${startY}"/>
  <hc:endPt x="${endX}" y="${endY}"/>
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:line>`;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:polygon> with hc:pt list conforming to OWPML PolygonType
   */
  private renderPolygonXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    points: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>;
    colorHex: string;
    width: string;
    style: string;
    fillBrushXml: string;
    drawTextXml: string;
  }): string {
    const safeColor = cleanHex(p.colorHex, "000000");
    const safeWidth = /^\d+$/.test(String(p.width).trim()) ? String(p.width).trim() : "0";
    const safeStyle = ["NONE", "SOLID", "DASH", "DOT", "DASH_DOT", "DASH_DOT_DOT"].includes(p.style)
      ? p.style
      : "NONE";

    const closedPts = [...p.points];
    if (closedPts.length >= 3) {
      const first = closedPts[0];
      const last = closedPts[closedPts.length - 1];
      if (first.x !== last.x || first.y !== last.y) {
        closedPts.push({ x: first.x, y: first.y });
      }
    }

    const ptXml = closedPts.map((pt) => `<hc:pt x="${pt.x}" y="${pt.y}"/>`).join("\n  ");

    return `<hp:polygon id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(p.w / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(p.h / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${safeColor}" width="${safeWidth}" style="${safeStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  ${p.fillBrushXml}${p.drawTextXml}
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  ${ptXml}
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:polygon>`;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:pic> with nested imgRect points, imgClip, inMargin, imgDim, and hc:img
   */
  private renderPicXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    binId: string;
    clipLeft: number;
    clipRight: number;
    clipTop: number;
    clipBottom: number;
    dimW: number;
    dimH: number;
  }): string {
    return `<hp:pic id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="PICTURE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}" reverse="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(p.w / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(p.h / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#000000" width="0" style="NONE" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:imgRect>
    <hc:pt0 x="0" y="0"/>
    <hc:pt1 x="${p.w}" y="0"/>
    <hc:pt2 x="${p.w}" y="${p.h}"/>
    <hc:pt3 x="0" y="${p.h}"/>
  </hp:imgRect>
  <hp:imgClip left="${p.clipLeft}" right="${p.clipRight}" top="${p.clipTop}" bottom="${p.clipBottom}"/>
  <hp:inMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
  <hp:imgDim dimwidth="${p.dimW}" dimheight="${p.dimH}"/>
  <hc:img binaryItemIDRef="${p.binId}" bright="0" contrast="0" effect="REAL_PIC" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:effects/>
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:pic>`;
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.ts
================================================================================
import JSZip from "jszip";
import {
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasPage,
  CanvasElement,
  CanvasTextRun,
  CanvasResource,
  ptToTwip,
} from "../types.js";

export interface DocxCanvasRendererOptions {
  creator?: string;
}

function escapeXml(str: string): string {
  if (!str) return "";
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&apos;")
    .replace(/[\x00-\x08\x0B\x0C\x0E-\x1F]/g, "");
}

function cleanHex(color?: string, fallback: string = "000000"): string {
  if (!color) return fallback;
  return color.replace("#", "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

export function resolveEastAsiaFont(fontFamily?: string): { ascii: string; eastAsia: string } {
  if (!fontFamily) {
    return { ascii: "Pretendard", eastAsia: "맑은 고딕" };
  }
  const f = fontFamily.trim();
  const isKoreanFont =
    f.includes("맑은 고딕") ||
    f.includes("Malgun") ||
    f.includes("Pretendard") ||
    f.includes("Noto Sans KR") ||
    f.includes("Nanum") ||
    f.includes("나눔") ||
    f.includes("돋움") ||
    f.includes("Dotum") ||
    f.includes("굴림") ||
    f.includes("Gulim") ||
    f.includes("바탕") ||
    f.includes("Batang") ||
    f.includes("Gungsuh") ||
    f.includes("KoPub");

  if (isKoreanFont) {
    return { ascii: f, eastAsia: f };
  }
  return { ascii: f, eastAsia: "맑은 고딕" };
}

export class DocxCanvasRenderer {
  private creator: string;

  constructor(options?: DocxCanvasRendererOptions) {
    this.creator = options?.creator || "Figma2Word Canvas Engine";
  }

  public async buildZip(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<JSZip> {
    const zip = new JSZip();

    // 1. [Content_Types].xml
    zip.file("[Content_Types].xml", this.buildContentTypesXml(doc));

    // 2. _rels/.rels
    zip.file("_rels/.rels", this.buildRootRelsXml());

    // 3. docProps/core.xml & app.xml
    zip.file("docProps/core.xml", this.buildCorePropsXml(doc));
    zip.file("docProps/app.xml", this.buildAppPropsXml(doc));

    // 4. word/styles.xml & settings.xml
    zip.file("word/styles.xml", this.buildStylesXml());
    zip.file("word/settings.xml", this.buildSettingsXml());

    // 5. word/media/
    const imageRelMap = new Map<string, string>(); // resourceId -> rId
    let rIdCounter = 2; // rId1 is styles.xml

    for (const [resId, res] of Object.entries(doc.resources || {})) {
      const rId = `rId${rIdCounter++}`;
      imageRelMap.set(resId, rId);
      zip.file(`word/media/${resId}`, res.data);
    }

    // 6. word/_rels/document.xml.rels
    zip.file(
      "word/_rels/document.xml.rels",
      this.buildDocumentRelsXml(imageRelMap)
    );

    // 7. word/document.xml
    zip.file("word/document.xml", this.buildDocumentXml(doc, imageRelMap));

    return zip;
  }

  public async renderToUint8Array(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    const zip = await this.buildZip(doc);
    return await zip.generateAsync({
      type: "uint8array",
      compression: "DEFLATE",
      compressionOptions: { level: 6 },
    });
  }

  public async renderToBlob(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Blob> {
    const uint8 = await this.renderToUint8Array(doc);
    return new Blob([uint8 as any], {
      type: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
    });
  }

  public async renderToBuffer(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Buffer> {
    const uint8 = await this.renderToUint8Array(doc);
    return Buffer.from(uint8.buffer, uint8.byteOffset, uint8.byteLength);
  }

  private buildContentTypesXml(doc: CanvasDocument): string {
    const mediaExtensions = new Set<string>(["png", "jpeg", "jpg"]);
    let defaultEntries = "";
    for (const ext of mediaExtensions) {
      const mime = ext === "png" ? "image/png" : "image/jpeg";
      defaultEntries += `<Default Extension="${ext}" ContentType="${mime}"/>`;
    }

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types">
  <Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/>
  <Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/>
  ${defaultEntries}
  <Override PartName="/word/document.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"/>
  <Override PartName="/word/styles.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.styles+xml"/>
  <Override PartName="/word/settings.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.settings+xml"/>
  <Override PartName="/docProps/core.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.core-properties+xml"/>
  <Override PartName="/docProps/app.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.extended-properties+xml"/>
</Types>`.trim();
  }

  private buildRootRelsXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">
  <Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="word/document.xml"/>
  <Relationship Id="rId2" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships/metadata/core-properties" Target="docProps/core.xml"/>
  <Relationship Id="rId3" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/extended-properties" Target="docProps/app.xml"/>
</Relationships>`.trim();
  }

  private buildCorePropsXml(doc: CanvasDocument): string {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<cp:coreProperties xmlns:cp="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/metadata/core-properties"
                   xmlns:dc="http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/"
                   xmlns:dcterms="http://purl.org/dc/terms/"
                   xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance">
  <dc:title>${escapeXml(doc.title)}</dc:title>
  <dc:creator>${escapeXml(this.creator)}</dc:creator>
  <cp:lastModifiedBy>${escapeXml(this.creator)}</cp:lastModifiedBy>
  <dcterms:created xsi:type="dcterms:W3CDTF">${now}</dcterms:created>
  <dcterms:modified xsi:type="dcterms:W3CDTF">${now}</dcterms:modified>
</cp:coreProperties>`.trim();
  }

  private buildAppPropsXml(doc: CanvasDocument): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<Properties xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/extended-properties">
  <Application>${escapeXml(this.creator)}</Application>
  <Pages>${doc.pages.length}</Pages>
</Properties>`.trim();
  }

  private buildStylesXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<w:styles xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main">
  <w:docDefaults>
    <w:rPrDefault>
      <w:rPr>
        <w:rFonts w:ascii="Inter" w:hAnsi="Inter" w:eastAsia="맑은 고딕" w:cs="Inter"/>
        <w:sz w:val="20"/>
        <w:szCs w:val="20"/>
        <w:lang w:val="ko-KR" w:eastAsia="ko-KR" w:bidi="ar-SA"/>
      </w:rPr>
    </w:rPrDefault>
    <w:pPrDefault>
      <w:pPr>
        <w:spacing w:after="0" w:line="240" w:lineRule="auto"/>
      </w:pPr>
    </w:pPrDefault>
  </w:docDefaults>
  <w:style w:type="paragraph" w:default="1" w:styleId="Normal">
    <w:name w:val="Normal"/>
    <w:qFormat/>
  </w:style>
</w:styles>`.trim();
  }

  private buildSettingsXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<w:settings xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main">
  <w:defaultTabStop w:val="720"/>
</w:settings>`.trim();
  }

  private buildDocumentRelsXml(imageRelMap: Map<string, string>): string {
    let rels = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">
  <Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/styles" Target="styles.xml"/>
  <Relationship Id="rIdSettings" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/settings" Target="settings.xml"/>`;

    for (const [resId, rId] of imageRelMap.entries()) {
      rels += `\n  <Relationship Id="${rId}" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/image" Target="media/${resId}"/>`;
    }

    rels += "\n</Relationships>";
    return rels.trim();
  }

  private buildDocumentXml(
    doc: CanvasDocument,
    imageRelMap: Map<string, string>
  ): string {
    const widthTwips = ptToTwip(doc.pageSize?.widthPt || 595.28);
    const heightTwips = ptToTwip(doc.pageSize?.heightPt || 841.89);

    const pageParagraphs: string[] = [];

    for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < doc.pages.length; pIdx++) {
      const page = doc.pages[pIdx];
      const runsXml: string[] = [];

      const pageWidthPt = page.bounds.width || doc.pageSize.widthPt;
      const pageHeightPt = page.bounds.height || doc.pageSize.heightPt;

      // 1. If page has custom background color, inject full-page background rect using per-page dimensions
      if (page.backgroundColor) {
        runsXml.push(
          this.buildBackgroundRect(
            pIdx,
            page.backgroundColor,
            pageWidthPt,
            pageHeightPt
          )
        );
      }

      // 2. Render all page elements
      for (let eIdx = 0; eIdx < page.elements.length; eIdx++) {
        const elem = page.elements[eIdx];
        const elemXml = this.renderElement(
          pIdx,
          eIdx,
          elem,
          imageRelMap,
          doc.resources
        );
        if (elemXml) {
          runsXml.push(elemXml);
        }
      }

      const curWidthTwips = ptToTwip(pageWidthPt);
      const curHeightTwips = ptToTwip(pageHeightPt);
      const curIsLandscape = pageWidthPt > pageHeightPt;
      const curOrient = curIsLandscape ? "landscape" : "portrait";

      const nextPage = pIdx < doc.pages.length - 1 ? doc.pages[pIdx + 1] : undefined;
      const nextWidthPt = nextPage ? (nextPage.bounds.width || doc.pageSize.widthPt) : undefined;
      const nextHeightPt = nextPage ? (nextPage.bounds.height || doc.pageSize.heightPt) : undefined;
      const nextDiffers =
        nextPage !== undefined &&
        (Math.abs(pageWidthPt - nextWidthPt!) > 1 || Math.abs(pageHeightPt - nextHeightPt!) > 1);

      let pPrXml = "";
      if (nextDiffers) {
        pPrXml = `<w:pPr>
  <w:spacing w:before="0" w:after="0" w:line="20" w:lineRule="exact"/>
  <w:autoSpaceDE w:val="0"/>
  <w:autoSpaceDN w:val="0"/>
  <w:sectPr>
    <w:type w:val="nextPage"/>
    <w:pgSz w:w="${curWidthTwips}" w:h="${curHeightTwips}" w:orient="${curOrient}"/>
    <w:pgMar w:top="0" w:right="0" w:bottom="0" w:left="0" w:header="0" w:footer="0" w:gutter="0"/>
  </w:sectPr>
</w:pPr>`;
      } else {
        const pageBreakBeforeXml =
          pIdx > 0 ? "<w:pageBreakBefore/>" : '<w:pageBreakBefore w:val="0"/>';
        pPrXml = `<w:pPr>
  ${pageBreakBeforeXml}
  <w:spacing w:before="0" w:after="0" w:line="20" w:lineRule="exact"/>
  <w:autoSpaceDE w:val="0"/>
  <w:autoSpaceDN w:val="0"/>
</w:pPr>`;
      }

      pageParagraphs.push(`<w:p>
  ${pPrXml}
  ${runsXml.join("\n  ")}
</w:p>`);
    }

    // Section properties (dynamic size and orientation for final section)
    const lastPage = doc.pages.length > 0 ? doc.pages[doc.pages.length - 1] : undefined;
    const lastWidthPt = lastPage ? (lastPage.bounds.width || doc.pageSize.widthPt) : doc.pageSize.widthPt;
    const lastHeightPt = lastPage ? (lastPage.bounds.height || doc.pageSize.heightPt) : doc.pageSize.heightPt;
    const finalWidthTwips = ptToTwip(lastWidthPt);
    const finalHeightTwips = ptToTwip(lastHeightPt);
    const isLandscape = lastWidthPt > lastHeightPt;
    const orient = isLandscape ? "landscape" : "portrait";
    const sectPrXml = `<w:sectPr>
  <w:pgSz w:w="${finalWidthTwips}" w:h="${finalHeightTwips}" w:orient="${orient}"/>
  <w:pgMar w:top="0" w:right="0" w:bottom="0" w:left="0" w:header="0" w:footer="0" w:gutter="0"/>
</w:sectPr>`;

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<w:document xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main"
            xmlns:v="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:vml"
            xmlns:o="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office"
            xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"
            xmlns:w10="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:word"
            xmlns:wp="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/wordprocessingDrawing">
  <w:body>
    ${pageParagraphs.join("\n")}
    ${sectPrXml}
  </w:body>
</w:document>`.trim();
  }

  private buildBackgroundRect(
    pageIdx: number,
    colorHex: string,
    widthPt: number,
    heightPt: number
  ): string {
    const hex = cleanHex(colorHex, "FFFFFF");
    return `<w:r>
  <w:pict>
    <v:rect id="bg_${pageIdx}"
            style="position:absolute;left:0;top:0;margin-left:0pt;margin-top:0pt;width:${widthPt.toFixed(
              3
            )}pt;height:${heightPt.toFixed(
      3
    )}pt;z-index:0;mso-position-horizontal-relative:page;mso-position-vertical-relative:page;mso-wrap-style:none"
            filled="t"
            stroked="f"
            fillcolor="#${hex}"/>
  </w:pict>
</w:r>`;
  }

  private renderElement(
    pageIdx: number,
    elemIdx: number,
    elem: CanvasElement,
    imageRelMap: Map<string, string>,
    resources?: Record<string, CanvasResource>
  ): string {
    const shapeId = `s_${pageIdx}_${elemIdx}`;
    const { x, y, width, height } = elem.bounds;
    const z = elem.zIndex;

    let rotDeg = 0;
    if (typeof elem.rotation === "number" && elem.rotation !== 0) {
      // If rotation is in radians (<= 2*PI) vs degrees, only apply to non-line elements
      rotDeg =
        Math.abs(elem.rotation) <= Math.PI * 2 + 0.01 && Math.abs(elem.rotation) < 6.5
          ? (-elem.rotation * 180) / Math.PI
          : elem.rotation;
    }
    const rotStyle =
      elem.type !== "line" && Math.abs(rotDeg) > 0.1
        ? `;rotation:${rotDeg.toFixed(2)}`
        : "";

    const hasText = !!(elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0);

    // Precision typography wrapping:
    // Only elements designed as multi-line paragraphs or containing explicit newlines should auto-wrap.
    // Single-line labels, metrics, chips, badges, button texts, and auto-width elements must NOT wrap,
    // preventing Word DirectWrite font variance from dropping words into invisible/awkward second lines.
    let wrapStyle = "mso-wrap-style:none";
    if (hasText) {
      const isAutoWidth = elem.textAutoResize === "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT";
      if (!isAutoWidth) {
        const hasHardNewline = elem.textRuns!.some((r) => r.text && /[\r\n]/.test(r.text));
        const maxFontSize = Math.max(...elem.textRuns!.map((r) => r.fontSizePt || 12));
        const firstLineHeight =
          elem.textRuns!.find((r) => r.lineHeightPt)?.lineHeightPt || maxFontSize * 1.25;
        const evalHeight = elem.contentHeightPt || elem.bounds.height;
        const estimatedLines = evalHeight / firstLineHeight;
        const totalChars = elem.textRuns!.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (r.text?.length || 0), 0);

        const isMultiLineBox =
          estimatedLines >= 1.45 ||
          hasHardNewline ||
          (totalChars >= 80 && elem.textAutoResize === "HEIGHT");

        if (isMultiLineBox) {
          wrapStyle = "mso-wrap-style:square";
        }
      }
    }

    const baseStyle = `position:absolute;left:0;top:0;margin-left:${x.toFixed(
      3
    )}pt;margin-top:${y.toFixed(3)}pt;width:${width.toFixed(
      3
    )}pt;height:${height.toFixed(
      3
    )}pt;z-index:${z};mso-position-horizontal-relative:page;mso-position-vertical-relative:page;${wrapStyle}${rotStyle}`;

    const altAttr = elem.id ? `alt="${escapeXml(elem.id)}"` : "";
    const titleAttr = `o:title="${escapeXml(elem.name || elem.id || "")}"`;

    // 1. Line
    if (elem.type === "line") {
      const strokeColor = cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor, "000000");
      const strokeW = elem.style.strokeWidth ? elem.style.strokeWidth.toFixed(2) : "1.00";
      const lineSpanX = width < 1 && height > 1 ? 0 : width;
      const lineSpanY = height <= 1 && width > 1 ? 0 : height;
      const dashChild = elem.style.strokeDashed ? '<v:stroke dashstyle="dash"/>' : "";
      return `<w:r>
  <w:pict>
    <v:line id="${shapeId}"
            ${altAttr}
            ${titleAttr}
            from="${x.toFixed(3)}pt,${y.toFixed(3)}pt"
            to="${(x + lineSpanX).toFixed(3)}pt,${(y + lineSpanY).toFixed(3)}pt"
            style="position:absolute;z-index:${z};mso-position-horizontal-relative:page;mso-position-vertical-relative:page"
            strokecolor="#${strokeColor}"
            strokeweight="${strokeW}pt">${dashChild}</v:line>
  </w:pict>
</w:r>`;
    }

    // 1b. Vector Shape
    if (elem.type === "vector") {
      const allOpenSubpaths =
        elem.vectorSubpaths &&
        elem.vectorSubpaths.length > 0 &&
        elem.vectorSubpaths.every((sp) => !sp.closed);
      const hasFill = !!elem.style.fillColor && elem.style.fillColor !== "none" && !allOpenSubpaths;
      const hasStroke = !!elem.style.strokeColor && (elem.style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;
      const filledAttr = hasFill ? 'filled="t"' : 'filled="f"';
      const fillcolorAttr = hasFill ? `fillcolor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor)}"` : "";
      const strokedAttr = hasStroke ? 'stroked="t"' : 'stroked="f"';
      const strokecolorAttr = hasStroke ? `strokecolor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor)}"` : "";
      const strokeweightAttr = hasStroke
        ? `strokeweight="${(elem.style.strokeWidth || 1).toFixed(2)}pt"`
        : "";

      const pathStr = elem.vectorPath || "";
      if (!pathStr) {
        return "";
      }

      const dashChild = hasStroke && elem.style.strokeDashed ? '<v:stroke dashstyle="dash"/>' : "";
      const opacityChild =
        hasFill && elem.style.fillOpacity !== undefined && elem.style.fillOpacity < 1
          ? `<v:fill opacity="${elem.style.fillOpacity.toFixed(2)}"/>`
          : "";

      if (dashChild || opacityChild) {
        return `<w:r>
  <w:pict>
    <v:shape id="${shapeId}"
             ${altAttr}
             ${titleAttr}
             style="${baseStyle}"
             coordsize="1000,1000"
             path="${pathStr}"
             ${filledAttr}
             ${fillcolorAttr}
             ${strokedAttr}
             ${strokecolorAttr}
             ${strokeweightAttr}>${dashChild}${opacityChild}</v:shape>
  </w:pict>
</w:r>`;
      }

      return `<w:r>
  <w:pict>
    <v:shape id="${shapeId}"
             ${altAttr}
             ${titleAttr}
             style="${baseStyle}"
             coordsize="1000,1000"
             path="${pathStr}"
             ${filledAttr}
             ${fillcolorAttr}
             ${strokedAttr}
             ${strokecolorAttr}
             ${strokeweightAttr}/>
  </w:pict>
</w:r>`;
    }

    // 2. Image
    if (elem.type === "image" && elem.imageRef) {
      const relId = imageRelMap.get(elem.imageRef) || "rId1";
      let cropAttr = "";
      let imgX = x;
      let imgY = y;
      let imgBoxW = width;
      let imgBoxH = height;

      const res = resources && elem.imageRef ? resources[elem.imageRef] : undefined;
      let imgW = 0;
      let imgH = 0;
      if (res && res.data && res.data.length > 24) {
        const d = res.data;
        const readU32 = (off: number) =>
          ((d[off] << 24) >>> 0) + (d[off + 1] << 16) + (d[off + 2] << 8) + d[off + 3];
        const readU16 = (off: number) =>
          (d[off] << 8) | d[off + 1];

        // PNG check: 89 50 4E 47
        if (
          d[0] === 0x89 &&
          d[1] === 0x50 &&
          d[2] === 0x4e &&
          d[3] === 0x47
        ) {
          imgW = readU32(16);
          imgH = readU32(20);
        } else if (d[0] === 0xff && d[1] === 0xd8) {
          // Basic JPEG SOF0/SOF2 marker parser
          let offset = 2;
          while (offset < d.length - 8) {
            const marker = readU16(offset);
            offset += 2;
            if (marker === 0xffc0 || marker === 0xffc2) {
              imgH = readU16(offset + 3);
              imgW = readU16(offset + 5);
              break;
            }
            const len = readU16(offset);
            offset += len;
          }
        }
      }

      if (elem.imageCrop) {
        const ct = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.top)) * 65536);
        const cb = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.bottom)) * 65536);
        const cl = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.left)) * 65536);
        const cr = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.right)) * 65536);
        const parts: string[] = [];
        if (ct > 0) parts.push(`croptop="${ct}f"`);
        if (cb > 0) parts.push(`cropbottom="${cb}f"`);
        if (cl > 0) parts.push(`cropleft="${cl}f"`);
        if (cr > 0) parts.push(`cropright="${cr}f"`);
        if (parts.length > 0) {
          cropAttr = " " + parts.join(" ");
        }
      } else if (elem.imageScaleMode === "FIT" && imgW > 0 && imgH > 0 && width > 0 && height > 0) {
        const arImg = imgW / imgH;
        const arBox = width / height;
        if (arImg > arBox) {
          imgBoxW = width;
          imgBoxH = width / arImg;
          imgY = y + (height - imgBoxH) / 2;
        } else {
          imgBoxH = height;
          imgBoxW = height * arImg;
          imgX = x + (width - imgBoxW) / 2;
        }
      } else if (imgW > 0 && imgH > 0 && width > 0 && height > 0) {
        const arImg = imgW / imgH;
        const arBox = width / height;
        if (arImg < arBox * 0.99) {
          // Image is taller than container box: crop top & bottom (object-fit: cover)
          const cropY = (1.0 - arImg / arBox) / 2.0;
          const cropVal = Math.round(cropY * 65536);
          cropAttr = ` croptop="${cropVal}f" cropbottom="${cropVal}f"`;
        } else if (arImg > arBox * 1.01) {
          // Image is wider than container box: crop left & right
          const cropX = (1.0 - arBox / arImg) / 2.0;
          const cropVal = Math.round(cropX * 65536);
          cropAttr = ` cropleft="${cropVal}f" cropright="${cropVal}f"`;
        }
      }

      const imgStyle = `position:absolute;left:0;top:0;margin-left:${imgX.toFixed(
        3
      )}pt;margin-top:${imgY.toFixed(3)}pt;width:${imgBoxW.toFixed(
        3
      )}pt;height:${imgBoxH.toFixed(
        3
      )}pt;z-index:${z};mso-position-horizontal-relative:page;mso-position-vertical-relative:page;mso-wrap-style:none${rotStyle}`;

      return `<w:r>
  <w:pict>
    <v:shape id="${shapeId}"
             ${altAttr}
             ${titleAttr}
             style="${imgStyle}"
             filled="f"
             stroked="f">
      <v:imagedata r:id="${relId}"${cropAttr} o:title="${escapeXml(elem.name || "image")}"/>
    </v:shape>
  </w:pict>
</w:r>`;
    }

    // 3. Card or Text
    const isText = elem.type === "text" || (elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0);
    const hasFill = !!elem.style.fillColor && elem.style.fillColor !== "none";
    const hasStroke = !!elem.style.strokeColor && (elem.style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

    const filledAttr = hasFill ? 'filled="t"' : 'filled="f"';
    const fillcolorAttr = hasFill ? `fillcolor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor)}"` : "";

    const strokedAttr = hasStroke ? 'stroked="t"' : 'stroked="f"';
    const strokecolorAttr = hasStroke ? `strokecolor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor)}"` : "";
    const strokeweightAttr = hasStroke
      ? `strokeweight="${(elem.style.strokeWidth || 1).toFixed(2)}pt"`
      : "";

    // Corner radius or Oval
    const isOval = elem.type === "oval";
    const isRound = !isOval && !!elem.style.cornerRadius && elem.style.cornerRadius > 0;
    const tag = isOval ? "v:oval" : isRound ? "v:roundrect" : "v:rect";
    const minDim = Math.max(1e-3, Math.min(width, height));
    const arcsizeAttr = isRound
      ? `arcsize="${Math.min(0.5, (elem.style.cornerRadius || 0) / minDim).toFixed(3)}"`
      : "";

    let textboxContentXml = "";
    if (isText && elem.textRuns) {
      const jcVal = elem.paragraphAlign || "left";
      const runsXml: string[] = [];

      for (const run of elem.textRuns) {
        const { ascii, eastAsia } = resolveEastAsiaFont(run.fontFamily);
        const fontAscii = escapeXml(ascii);
        const fontEastAsia = escapeXml(eastAsia);
        const fontSizeHalfPt = Math.round((run.fontSizePt || 10) * 2);
        const colorHex = cleanHex(run.color, "000000");

        const boldXml = (run.fontWeight || 400) >= 600 ? "<w:b/>" : "";
        const italicXml = run.italic ? "<w:i/>" : "";
        const uXml = run.underline ? '<w:u w:val="single"/>' : "";
        const strikeXml = run.strike ? "<w:strike/>" : "";
        const letterSpacingTwips =
          typeof run.letterSpacingPt === "number" && run.letterSpacingPt !== 0
            ? ptToTwip(run.letterSpacingPt)
            : 0;
        const letterSpacingXml =
          letterSpacingTwips !== 0 ? `<w:spacing w:val="${letterSpacingTwips}"/>` : "";

        const lines = run.text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
        const textParts = lines.map((l) => `<w:t xml:space="preserve">${escapeXml(l)}</w:t>`);
        const textXml = textParts.join("<w:br/>");

        runsXml.push(`<w:r>
  <w:rPr>
    <w:rFonts w:ascii="${fontAscii}" w:hAnsi="${fontAscii}" w:eastAsia="${fontEastAsia}" w:cs="${fontAscii}"/>
    <w:sz w:val="${fontSizeHalfPt}"/>
    <w:szCs w:val="${fontSizeHalfPt}"/>
    <w:color w:val="${colorHex}"/>
    ${letterSpacingXml}
    ${boldXml}
    ${italicXml}
    ${uXml}
    ${strikeXml}
  </w:rPr>
  ${textXml}
</w:r>`);
      }

      const pad = elem.innerPaddingPt || {};
      const padTop = pad.top ?? 0;
      const padRight = pad.right ?? 0;
      const padBottom = pad.bottom ?? 0;
      const padLeft = pad.left ?? 0;
      const insetAttr = `inset="${padLeft.toFixed(2)}pt,${padTop.toFixed(2)}pt,${padRight.toFixed(2)}pt,${padBottom.toFixed(2)}pt"`;

      const firstLineHeight = elem.textRuns.find((r) => r.lineHeightPt)?.lineHeightPt;
      const maxFontSize = Math.max(...elem.textRuns.map((r) => r.fontSizePt || 12));
      const isPureText = elem.type === "text" && !hasFill && !hasStroke;
