import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../src/canvas/parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { CanvasDocument } from "../src/canvas/types.js";
import { TextCollisionDetector } from "../scripts/detect_text_collision.js";
import * as zipfile from "jszip";
import * as fs from "fs";

describe("Phase 23.1: Canonical Text Geometry & Overflow Recovery", () => {
  const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
  const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();

  it("Requirement 1: Serializes hh:paraPr with authentic OWPML attributes (snapToGrid=0, breakSetting BREAK_WORD, autoSpacing 0)", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Test ParaPr",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [{
        pageNumber: 1,
        name: "P1",
        bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
        elements: [{
          id: "t1",
          name: "Test Text",
          type: "text",
          bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 400, height: 50 },
          zIndex: 1,
          style: {},
          textRuns: [{ text: "테스트 한글 텍스트", fontSizePt: 10, lineHeightPt: 16, fontFamily: "맑은 고딕" }],
        }],
      }],
    };

    const buf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await zipfile.default.loadAsync(buf);
    const headerXml = await zip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");

    // Must have snapToGrid="0"
    expect(headerXml).toContain('snapToGrid="0"');
    // Must NOT have snapToGrid="1"
    expect(headerXml).not.toContain('snapToGrid="1"');
    // Must contain authentic breakSetting
    expect(headerXml).toContain('breakLatinWord="KEEP_WORD" breakNonLatinWord="BREAK_WORD"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lineWrap="BREAK"');
    // Must contain autoSpacing eAsianEng="0" eAsianNum="0"
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:autoSpacing eAsianEng="0" eAsianNum="0"/>');
    // Must contain zero paragraph margin
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hc:intent value="0" unit="HWPUNIT"/>');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hc:left value="0" unit="HWPUNIT"/>');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hc:right value="0" unit="HWPUNIT"/>');
  });

  it("Requirement 2: Sets textMargin left and right to 0 by default, avoiding artificial width curtailment", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Test TextMargin",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [{
        pageNumber: 1,
        name: "P1",
        bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
        elements: [{
          id: "t1",
          name: "Full Width Text",
          type: "text",
          bounds: { x: 40, y: 100, width: 500, height: 60 },
          zIndex: 1,
          style: { cornerRadius: 10 },
          textRuns: [{ text: "기본 마진 테스트", fontSizePt: 11, fontFamily: "맑은 고딕" }],
        }],
      }],
    };

    const buf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await zipfile.default.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // textMargin left and right must be 0
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:textMargin left="0" right="0"');
    // drawText lastWidth and subList textWidth must match shape width (500pt * 100 = 50000 HWPU)
    expect(secXml).toContain('lastWidth="50000"');
    expect(secXml).toContain('textWidth="50000"');
  });

  it("Requirement 3: Accurately sizes explicit innerPaddingPt when provided", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Test Explicit Padding",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [{
        pageNumber: 1,
        name: "P1",
        bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
        elements: [{
          id: "t1",
          name: "Padded Text",
          type: "text",
          bounds: { x: 40, y: 100, width: 400, height: 60 },
          innerPaddingPt: { top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 12, right: 12 },
          zIndex: 1,
          style: {},
          textRuns: [{ text: "패딩 테스트", fontSizePt: 10, fontFamily: "맑은 고딕" }],
        }],
      }],
    };

    const buf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await zipfile.default.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // 12pt * 100 = 1200 HWPU, 8pt * 100 = 800 HWPU
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:textMargin left="1200" right="1200" top="800" bottom="800"/>');
  });

  it("Requirement 4: Safely renders CanvasDocument with omitted or empty resources without throwing", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Resource Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [{
        pageNumber: 1,
        name: "P1",
        bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
        elements: [{
          id: "t1",
          name: "Text",
          type: "text",
          bounds: { x: 10, y: 10, width: 100, height: 20 },
          zIndex: 1,
          style: {},
          textRuns: [{ text: "OK", fontSizePt: 10 }],
        }],
      }],
      // resources explicitly omitted
    };

    const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    expect(hwpxBuf).toBeInstanceOf(Buffer);
    expect(hwpxBuf.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);

    const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    expect(docxBuf).toBeInstanceOf(Buffer);
    expect(docxBuf.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);
  });

  it("Requirement 5: Accurately detects text overflow and collision risk via TextCollisionDetector", () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Collision Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [{
        pageNumber: 1,
        name: "P1",
        bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
        elements: [
          {
            id: "t_lead",
            name: "Lead Text",
            type: "text",
            bounds: { x: 50, y: 100, width: 200, height: 20 }, // 20pt height is only enough for 1 line
            zIndex: 1,
            textAutoResize: "HEIGHT",
            style: {},
            textRuns: [{
              // Long text that requires 3 lines at 200pt width
              text: "이 문장은 200pt 폭에서 약 세 줄로 줄바꿈되어 최소 45pt의 높이를 필요로 하므로 할당된 20pt 높이를 초과합니다.",
              fontSizePt: 10,
              lineHeightPt: 15,
            }],
          },
          {
            id: "t_subtitle",
            name: "Downstream Subtitle",
            type: "text",
            bounds: { x: 50, y: 125, width: 200, height: 20 }, // Placed at y=125, colliding with line 2/3 of t_lead
            zIndex: 2,
            style: {},
            textRuns: [{ text: "소제목", fontSizePt: 12 }],
          },
        ],
      }],
    };

    const detector = new TextCollisionDetector();
    const records = detector.analyzeDocument(doc);

    expect(records.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    const overflowRec = records.find(r => r.sourceElementId === "t_lead" && (r.category === "B_VERTICAL_OVERFLOW" || r.category === "E_SOURCE_GEOMETRY_CONFLICT"));
    expect(overflowRec).toBeDefined();

    const collisionRec = records.find(r => r.category === "C_INTER_ELEMENT_COLLISION" && r.targetElementId === "t_subtitle");
    expect(collisionRec).toBeDefined();
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/hwpx-schema-conformance.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { CanvasDocument } from "../src/canvas/types.js";

describe("Phase 20: HWPX OWPML / KS X 6101 Schema Conformance & Recovery Suite", () => {
  it("strictly wraps hp:secPr inside hp:run charPrIDRef=0 and follows exact secPr child sequence", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "SecPr Structure Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "t1",
              type: "text",
              bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 30 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              textRuns: [{ text: "섹션 구조 검증", fontSizePt: 12 }],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // 1. hp:secPr MUST be inside hp:run inside the first hp:p
    expect(secXml).toMatch(/<hp:p\b[^>]*>\s*<hp:run charPrIDRef="0">\s*<hp:secPr\b/);

    // 2. hp:ctrl with hp:colPr must follow hp:secPr inside the same run
    expect(secXml).toMatch(/<\/hp:secPr>\s*<hp:ctrl>\s*<hp:colPr\b/);

    // 3. Child sequence inside hp:secPr:
    // grid -> startNum -> visibility -> lineNumberShape -> pagePr -> footNotePr -> endNotePr -> pageBorderFill
    const gridIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:grid");
    const startNumIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:startNum");
    const visIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:visibility");
    const lineNumIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:lineNumberShape");
    const pagePrIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:pagePr");
    const footIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:footNotePr");
    const endIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:endNotePr");
    const borderFillIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:pageBorderFill");

    expect(gridIdx).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(startNumIdx).toBeGreaterThan(gridIdx);
    expect(visIdx).toBeGreaterThan(startNumIdx);
    expect(lineNumIdx).toBeGreaterThan(visIdx);
    expect(pagePrIdx).toBeGreaterThan(lineNumIdx);
    expect(footIdx).toBeGreaterThan(pagePrIdx);
    expect(endIdx).toBeGreaterThan(footIdx);
    expect(borderFillIdx).toBeGreaterThan(endIdx);
  });

  it("strictly enforces child element order and unsignedInt ID for shapes", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Shape Order Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "rect1",
              type: "card",
              bounds: { x: 20, y: 20, width: 200, height: 100 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: { fillColor: "#EEEEEE", strokeColor: "#333333", strokeWidth: 1, cornerRadius: 8 },
              textRuns: [{ text: "도형 순서 검증", fontSizePt: 10 }],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // Shape ID and instid must be pure integers, NOT string paths like "1_1"
    const idMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:rect id="(\d+)"[^>]*instid="(\d+)"/);
    expect(idMatch).toBeDefined();
    expect(idMatch![1]).toBe(idMatch![2]);

    // Sequence inside hp:rect:
    // offset -> orgSz -> curSz -> flip -> rotationInfo -> renderingInfo -> lineShape -> fillBrush -> drawText -> shadow -> geometry (hc:pt0..3) -> sz -> pos -> outMargin
    const offsetIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:offset");
    const orgSzIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:orgSz");
    const curSzIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:curSz");
    const flipIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:flip");
    const rotIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:rotationInfo");
    const rendIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:renderingInfo");
    const lineShapeIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:lineShape");
    const fillBrushIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hc:fillBrush");
    const drawTextIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:drawText");
    const shadowIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:shadow");
    const pt0Idx = secXml.indexOf("<hc:pt0");
    const pt3Idx = secXml.indexOf("<hc:pt3");
    const szIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:sz");
    const posIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:pos");
    const outMarginIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:outMargin");

    expect(offsetIdx).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(orgSzIdx).toBeGreaterThan(offsetIdx);
    expect(curSzIdx).toBeGreaterThan(orgSzIdx);
    expect(flipIdx).toBeGreaterThan(curSzIdx);
    expect(rotIdx).toBeGreaterThan(flipIdx);
    expect(rendIdx).toBeGreaterThan(rotIdx);
    expect(lineShapeIdx).toBeGreaterThan(rendIdx);
    expect(fillBrushIdx).toBeGreaterThan(lineShapeIdx);
    expect(drawTextIdx).toBeGreaterThan(fillBrushIdx);
    expect(shadowIdx).toBeGreaterThan(drawTextIdx);
    expect(pt0Idx).toBeGreaterThan(shadowIdx);
    expect(pt3Idx).toBeGreaterThan(pt0Idx);
    expect(szIdx).toBeGreaterThan(pt3Idx);
    expect(posIdx).toBeGreaterThan(szIdx);
    expect(outMarginIdx).toBeGreaterThan(posIdx);
  });

  it("renders dedicated geometry for hp:ellipse conforming to KS X 6101 ellipse specification", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Oval Geometry Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "oval1",
              type: "oval",
              bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 80 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: { fillColor: "#00AAEE", strokeColor: "#003366", strokeWidth: 2 },
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // hp:ellipse tag and attributes (conforming to KS X 6101 and authentic Hancom corpus)
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:ellipse");
    expect(secXml).toContain('intervalDirty="0"');
    expect(secXml).toContain('hasArcPr="0"');
    expect(secXml).toContain('arcType="NORMAL"');

    // Dedicated oval geometry tags
    // W = 10000 HWPU, H = 8000 HWPU
    // center: (5000, 4000)
    // ax1: (10000, 4000)
    // ax2: (5000, 8000)
    // start1..end2: (10000, 4000)
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:center x="5000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:ax1 x="10000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:ax2 x="5000" y="8000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:start1 x="10000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:end1 x="10000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:start2 x="10000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:end2 x="10000" y="4000"/>');

    // Must also contain shadow, sz, pos, outMargin in proper sequence
    const centerIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hc:center");
    const szIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:sz", centerIdx);
    const posIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:pos", szIdx);
    const outMarginIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:outMargin", posIdx);

    expect(centerIdx).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(szIdx).toBeGreaterThan(centerIdx);
    expect(posIdx).toBeGreaterThan(szIdx);
    expect(outMarginIdx).toBeGreaterThan(posIdx);
  });

  it("renders hp:pic with nested imgRect points, imgClip, inMargin, and single binaryItemIDRef", async () => {
    const pngHeader = new Uint8Array([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d, 0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, // width: 256
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x80, // height: 128
      0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    ]);

    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Image Structure Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "img1",
              type: "image",
              bounds: { x: 10, y: 10, width: 200, height: 100 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              imageRef: "res_photo",
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      resources: {
        res_photo: {
          mimeType: "image/png",
          data: pngHeader,
        },
      },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // 1. Tag attributes
    expect(secXml).toMatch(/<hp:pic id="\d+" zOrder="1" numberingType="PICTURE" [^>]*reverse="0">/);

    // 2. imgRect with nested hc:pt0..3
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:imgRect>");
    expect(secXml).toMatch(/<hp:imgRect>\s*<hc:pt0 x="0" y="0"\/>\s*<hc:pt1\b/);

    // 3. Spurious inImgDim and binRef must NOT exist
    expect(secXml).not.toContain("<hp:inImgDim");
    expect(secXml).not.toContain('binRef=');

    // 4. Proper tags: imgClip, inMargin, imgDim, hc:img, effects, shadow, sz, pos, outMargin
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:imgClip");
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:inMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:imgDim");
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:img binaryItemIDRef="BIN0001" bright="0" contrast="0" effect="REAL_PIC" alpha="0"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:effects/>");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:shadow");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:sz");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:pos");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:outMargin");
  });

  it("renders uppercase fontface lang codes in Contents/header.xml", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Header Lang Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [],
        },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const headerXml = await zip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");

    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="HANGUL"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="LATIN"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="HANJA"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="JAPANESE"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="OTHER"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="SYMBOL"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="USER"');

    // Ensure lowercase was completely eliminated
    expect(headerXml).not.toContain('lang="hangul"');
    expect(headerXml).not.toContain('lang="latin"');
  });

  it("renders complete mandatory children in default charPr id=0 when charPrList is empty", async () => {
    const emptyDoc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Empty CharPr Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [{ pageNumber: 1, bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }, elements: [] }],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(emptyDoc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const headerXml = await zip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");

    // Must contain complete child set conforming to OWPML charPr schema
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:charPr id="0"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:fontRef hangul="0"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:ratio hangul="100"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:spacing hangul="0"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:relSz hangul="100"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:offset hangul="0"');
  });

  it("strictly sets secCnt='1' in header.xml for single-section multi-page documents", async () => {
    const multiPageDoc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Multi Page Section Count Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        { pageNumber: 1, bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }, elements: [] },
        { pageNumber: 2, bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }, elements: [] },
        { pageNumber: 3, bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }, elements: [] },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(multiPageDoc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const headerXml = await zip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");

    // secCnt must match actual section files in package (1), not page count
    expect(headerXml).toMatch(/<hh:head\b[^>]*secCnt="1"/);
  });
});



================================================================================
FILE: tests/phase18-architecture-fidelity.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../src/canvas/parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { DocxCanvasParser } from "../src/canvas/parser/docxCanvasParser.js";
import { SvgPath } from "../src/canvas/geometry/svgPathParser.js";
import { CanvasDocument } from "../src/canvas/types.js";

describe("Phase 18: Architecture Hardening & Real-World Fidelity Suite", () => {
  // =========================================================================
  // B-1: Multi-Page Mixed Orientation Background Geometry & DOCX->HWPX Roundtrip
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Mission B-1: Mixed Orientation Background Geometry & Roundtrip", () => {
    it("should size background rects per-page for mixed portrait/landscape documents and roundtrip DOCX -> HWPX", async () => {
      const mixedDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Mixed Orientation Roundtrip",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
            elements: [
              {
                id: "p1_card",
                name: "Portrait Card",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 40, y: 40, width: 200, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#EFF6FF", strokeColor: "#3B82F6", strokeWidth: 1, strokeDashed: true },
              },
            ],
          },
          {
            pageNumber: 2,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 841.89, height: 595.28 },
            backgroundColor: "#1E293B",
            elements: [
              {
                id: "p2_card",
                name: "Landscape Card",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 60, y: 60, width: 600, height: 300 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#334155", cornerRadius: 12 },
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      // Page 1 background rect must match Portrait (595.280pt x 841.890pt)
      expect(docXml).toContain('id="bg_0"');
      expect(docXml).toContain('width:595.280pt;height:841.890pt');
      // Page 2 background rect must match Landscape (841.890pt x 595.280pt)
      expect(docXml).toContain('id="bg_1"');
      expect(docXml).toContain('width:841.890pt;height:595.280pt');

      // Direct HWPX check: Page 2 background rect has width="84189" height="59528" and matching id/instid
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const directHwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const directHwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(directHwpxBuf);
      const directSecXml = await directHwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(directSecXml).toContain('<hp:orgSz width="84189" height="59528"/>');
      expect(directSecXml).toContain('faceColor="#1E293B"');

      // DOCX -> DocxCanvasParser -> HwpxCanvasRenderer Roundtrip
      const docxParser = new DocxCanvasParser();
      const parsedFromDocx = await docxParser.parse(docxBuf);
      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages.length).toBe(2);
      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages[0].bounds.width).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 1);
      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages[0].bounds.height).toBeCloseTo(841.89, 1);
      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages[0].backgroundColor).toBe("#FFFFFF");
      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages[0].elements[0].style.strokeDashed).toBe(true);

      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages[1].bounds.width).toBeCloseTo(841.89, 1);
      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages[1].bounds.height).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 1);
      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages[1].backgroundColor).toBe("#1E293B");
      expect(parsedFromDocx.pages[1].elements[0].style.cornerRadius).toBeCloseTo(12, 0);

      const roundtripHwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(parsedFromDocx);
      const rtZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(roundtripHwpxBuf);
      const rtSecXml = await rtZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(rtSecXml).toContain('landscape="WIDELY" width="59530" height="84190"');
      expect(rtSecXml).toContain('landscape="NARROWLY" width="59530" height="84190"');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // B-2: Typography, Line Height, Letter Spacing, Underline/Strike & Padding
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Mission B-2: Typography & Textbox Fidelity", () => {
    it("should preserve explicit lineHeightPx, letterSpacing, underline, strikethrough, and padding while ignoring INTRINSIC_% auto line-height", async () => {
      const figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
      const doc = figmaParser.parse({
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Typography Test",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [
          {
            id: "1:1",
            name: "Page 1",
            type: "FRAME",
            absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            children: [
              {
                id: "1:2",
                name: "Explicit Typography Block",
                type: "TEXT",
                characters: "Body text with explicit spacing",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 40, y: 40, width: 300, height: 80 },
                paddingLeft: 12,
                paddingTop: 8,
                paddingRight: 12,
                paddingBottom: 8,
                style: {
                  fontFamily: "Pretendard",
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  lineHeightPx: 18,
                  lineHeightUnit: "FONT_SIZE_%",
                  letterSpacing: -0.6,
                  textDecoration: "UNDERLINE",
                  textAlignHorizontal: "JUSTIFIED",
                  textAlignVertical: "CENTER",
                },
              },
              {
                id: "1:3",
                name: "Intrinsic Auto LineHeight Label",
                type: "TEXT",
                characters: "Single Line Label",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 40, y: 140, width: 200, height: 24 },
                style: {
                  fontFamily: "Pretendard",
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  lineHeightPx: 19.2,
                  lineHeightUnit: "INTRINSIC_%",
                  textDecoration: "STRIKETHROUGH",
                },
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
      } as any);

      const elemExplicit = doc.pages[0].elements[0];
      const elemIntrinsic = doc.pages[0].elements[1];

      expect(elemExplicit.innerPaddingPt).toEqual({ top: 8, right: 12, bottom: 8, left: 12 });
      expect(elemExplicit.textRuns![0].lineHeightPt).toBe(18);
      expect(elemExplicit.textRuns![0].letterSpacingPt).toBe(-0.6);
      expect(elemExplicit.textRuns![0].underline).toBe(true);

      // INTRINSIC_% should not force exact lineHeightPt
      expect(elemIntrinsic.textRuns![0].lineHeightPt).toBeUndefined();
      expect(elemIntrinsic.textRuns![0].strike).toBe(true);

      // Check DOCX XML output
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      // 18pt = 360 twips; -0.6pt = -12 twips
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:spacing w:before="0" w:after="0" w:line="360" w:lineRule="exact"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:spacing w:val="-12"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:u w:val="single"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:strike/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:contextualSpacing w:val="0"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:autoSpaceDE w:val="0"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:autoSpaceDN w:val="0"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('inset="12.00pt,8.00pt,12.00pt,8.00pt"');

      // Check HWPX XML output
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const headerXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");
      const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // HWPX custom paraPr with FIXED 1800 HWPUNIT lineSpacing
      expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:lineSpacing type="FIXED" value="1800" unit="HWPUNIT"/>');
      // HWPX charPr with underline, strikeout, and -5% letter spacing (-0.6 / 12 = -5%)
      expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:underline type="BOTTOM" shape="SOLID"');
      expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:strikeout shape="CONTINUOUS"');
      expect(headerXml).toContain('hangul="-5" latin="-5"');
      // HWPX textMargin
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:textMargin left="1200" right="1200" top="800" bottom="800"/>');

      // Verify DOCX -> DocxCanvasParser roundtrip recovers typography
      const docxParser = new DocxCanvasParser();
      const rtDoc = await docxParser.parse(docxBuf);
      const rtElem = rtDoc.pages[0].elements[0];
      expect(rtElem.verticalAlign).toBe("center");
      expect(rtElem.innerPaddingPt).toEqual({ top: 8, right: 12, bottom: 8, left: 12 });
      expect(rtElem.textRuns![0].lineHeightPt).toBe(18);
      expect(rtElem.textRuns![0].letterSpacingPt).toBe(-0.6);
      expect(rtElem.textRuns![0].underline).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // B-3: Image Scale Modes (FILL, FIT, CROP with imageTransform)
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Mission B-3: Image Scale Modes (FILL, FIT, CROP)", () => {
    // 200x100 wide PNG header
    const widePngBase64 =
      "data:image/png;base64," +
      Buffer.from([
        0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d,
        0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xc8, // width = 200
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // height = 100
        0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
      ]).toString("base64");

    it("should extract imageTransform CROP matrix and FIT scaleMode and render accurately in DOCX and HWPX", async () => {
      const figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser({
        imageMap: { img_wide: widePngBase64 },
      });

      const doc = figmaParser.parse({
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Image Modes Test",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [
          {
            id: "1:1",
            name: "Page 1",
            type: "FRAME",
            absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            children: [
              {
                id: "1:2",
                name: "Cropped Image",
                type: "RECTANGLE",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                fills: [
                  {
                    type: "IMAGE",
                    scaleMode: "CROP",
                    imageRef: "img_wide",
                    imageTransform: [
                      [0.5, 0, 0.25],
                      [0, 0.5, 0.1],
                    ],
                  },
                ],
              },
              {
                id: "1:3",
                name: "Fitted Image",
                type: "RECTANGLE",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 200, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                fills: [
                  {
                    type: "IMAGE",
                    scaleMode: "FIT",
                    imageRef: "img_wide",
                  },
                ],
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
      } as any);

      const cropElem = doc.pages[0].elements[0];
      const fitElem = doc.pages[0].elements[1];

      expect(cropElem.imageScaleMode).toBe("CROP");
      expect(cropElem.imageCrop).toEqual({
        left: 0.25,
        right: 0.25,
        top: 0.1,
        bottom: 0.4,
      });
      expect(fitElem.imageScaleMode).toBe("FIT");

      // Render DOCX
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      // CROP in DOCX: left=0.25*65536=16384f, right=16384f, top=0.1*65536=6554f, bottom=0.4*65536=26214f
      expect(docXml).toContain('croptop="6554f" cropbottom="26214f" cropleft="16384f" cropright="16384f"');
      // FIT in DOCX: 200x100 image inside 100x100 box -> letterboxed to width:100.000pt;height:50.000pt at margin-top:75.000pt
      expect(docXml).toContain("margin-left:200.000pt;margin-top:75.000pt;width:100.000pt;height:50.000pt");

      // Render HWPX
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // dimW = 200 * 75 = 15000, dimH = 100 * 75 = 7500
      // CROP in HWPX: left = 15000 * 0.25 = 3750, right = 15000 * 0.75 = 11250, top = 7500 * 0.1 = 750, bottom = 7500 * 0.6 = 4500
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:imgClip left="3750" right="11250" top="750" bottom="4500"/>');
      // FIT in HWPX: unclipped (0..15000, 0..7500) with orgSz width="10000" height="5000" and vertOffset="7500"
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:imgClip left="0" right="15000" top="0" bottom="7500"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:orgSz width="10000" height="5000"/>');

      // DOCX -> DocxCanvasParser roundtrip recovers imageCrop
      const docxParser = new DocxCanvasParser();
      const rtDoc = await docxParser.parse(docxBuf);
      const rtCropElem = rtDoc.pages[0].elements[0];
      expect(rtCropElem.imageScaleMode).toBe("CROP");
      expect(rtCropElem.imageCrop?.left).toBeCloseTo(0.25, 2);
      expect(rtCropElem.imageCrop?.bottom).toBeCloseTo(0.4, 2);
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // B-4: Donut Chart ELLIPSE arcData, Multi-Subpath VML & Rotated LINE
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Mission B-4: Donut Chart ELLIPSE arcData & Vector Geometry", () => {
    it("should convert Figma ELLIPSE with arcData into exact cubic Bezier donut sector vectors", async () => {
      const figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
      const doc = figmaParser.parse({
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Donut Chart Test",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [
          {
            id: "1:1",
            name: "Page 1",
            type: "FRAME",
            absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            children: [
              {
                id: "7:275",
                name: "Donut Segment 1 (36%)",
                type: "ELLIPSE",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 76.5, y: 423, width: 140, height: 140 },
                fills: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.23, g: 0.51, b: 0.96 } }],
                arcData: {
                  startingAngle: -1.5707963705062866,
                  endingAngle: 0.691150426864624,
                  innerRadius: 0.6499999761581421,
                },
              },
              {
                id: "7:279",
                name: "Normal Full Circle",
                type: "ELLIPSE",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 300, y: 423, width: 50, height: 50 },
                fills: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.1, g: 0.8, b: 0.4 } }],
                arcData: {
                  startingAngle: 0,
                  endingAngle: 6.2831854820251465,
                  innerRadius: 0,
                },
              },
              {
                id: "7:280",
                name: "Rotated Vertical Divider",
                type: "LINE",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 250, y: 100, width: 80, height: 0 },
                rotation: 90,
                strokes: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.8, g: 0.8, b: 0.8 } }],
                strokeWeight: 1.5,
                strokeDashes: [4, 4],
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
      } as any);

      const donutSeg = doc.pages[0].elements[0];
      const fullOval = doc.pages[0].elements[1];
      const vertLine = doc.pages[0].elements[2];

      // Donut segment MUST be a vector with Bezier curve path, NOT an oval!
      expect(donutSeg.type).toBe("vector");
      expect(donutSeg.rawSvgPath).toContain("C");
      expect(donutSeg.rawSvgPath).toContain("Z");
      expect(donutSeg.vectorPath).toContain("m 500,0 c 694,0,870,112,952,287");

      // Full circle with innerRadius=0 and sweep=2*PI remains native oval
      expect(fullOval.type).toBe("oval");

      // Rotated 90-deg line becomes vertical (width=0, height=80) with dashed stroke
      expect(vertLine.type).toBe("line");
      expect(vertLine.bounds.width).toBe(0);
      expect(vertLine.bounds.height).toBeCloseTo(80, 1);
      expect(vertLine.style.strokeDashed).toBe(true);

      // Render DOCX & verify VML
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      expect(docXml).toContain('alt="7:275"');
      expect(docXml).toContain("m 500,0 c 694,0,870,112,952,287");
      expect(docXml).toContain('<v:stroke dashstyle="dash"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('from="250.000pt,100.000pt"');
      expect(docXml).toContain('to="250.000pt,180.000pt"');
    });

    it("should not emit premature 'e' path termination in multi-subpath VML vectors", () => {
      const parsed = SvgPath.parse("M 0 0 L 40 0 L 40 40 L 0 40 Z M 60 60 L 100 60 L 100 100 L 60 100 Z");
      const vml = parsed.toVmlPath(1000, 1000);

      // 'e' must appear ONLY once at the very end of the VML path string
      const eMatches = vml.match(/\be\b/g);
      expect(eMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(eMatches!.length).toBe(1);
      expect(vml.endsWith("x e")).toBe(true);
      expect(vml).not.toContain("x e m");
    });

    it("should convert all 4 KPI Donut Chart segments in Customer Report1-Live-Snapshot.json Page 4 into vectors", () => {
      const report1Path = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "debug/customer/Report1-Live-Snapshot.json");
      if (!fs.existsSync(report1Path)) return;

      const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(report1Path, "utf-8"));
      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
      const doc = parser.parse(raw);

      expect(doc.pages.length).toBe(9);
      const page4 = doc.pages[3];
      const donutIds = ["7:275", "7:276", "7:277", "7:278"];
      for (const id of donutIds) {
        const seg = page4.elements.find((e) => e.id === id);
        expect(seg).toBeDefined();
        expect(seg!.type).toBe("vector");
        expect(seg!.vectorPath).toContain("c ");
      }
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // Mission E-1 & E-3: Corrupt Input Error Handling & Mutation Sensitivity
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Mission E-1 & E-3: Corrupt Input Handling & Mutation Verification Sensitivity", () => {
    it("should throw clear descriptive errors for invalid/corrupt Figma JSON and DOCX inputs", async () => {
      const figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
      expect(() => figmaParser.parse(null as any)).toThrow(/Invalid Figma JSON input/);
      expect(() => figmaParser.parse({ foo: "bar" } as any)).toThrow(/Invalid Figma JSON structure/);

      const docxParser = new DocxCanvasParser();
      const emptyZip = new JSZip();
      emptyZip.file("hello.txt", "not a docx");
      const badBuf = await emptyZip.generateAsync({ type: "nodebuffer" });
      await expect(docxParser.parse(badBuf)).rejects.toThrow(/word\/document\.xml missing/);
    });

    it("should detect broken VML multi-subpath termination, closed-polygon open paths, and uncropped images", () => {
      // Validator functions used across our audit suite
      const isValidMultiSubpathVml = (vml: string) => {
        const eCount = (vml.match(/\be\b/g) || []).length;
        const mCount = (vml.match(/\bm\b/g) || []).length;
        return mCount >= 2 && eCount === 1 && !vml.includes("x e m");
      };

      const isValidOpenPathHwpx = (secXml: string) => {
        return secXml.includes("<hp:line") && !secXml.includes("<hp:polygon");
      };

      const isValidDonutArcElement = (elem: { type: string; vectorPath?: string }) => {
        return elem.type === "vector" && !!elem.vectorPath && elem.vectorPath.includes("c ");
      };

      // 1. Prove valid outputs pass
      const goodVml = SvgPath.parse("M 0 0 L 10 0 L 10 10 Z M 20 20 L 30 20 L 30 30 Z").toVmlPath(1000, 1000);
      expect(isValidMultiSubpathVml(goodVml)).toBe(true);

      // 2. Prove mutated/broken outputs ("x e m", <hp:polygon> on open path, <v:oval> on donut) FAIL
      const mutatedBrokenVml = goodVml.replace("x m", "x e m");
      expect(isValidMultiSubpathVml(mutatedBrokenVml)).toBe(false);

      const mutatedBrokenOpenPathHwpx = `<hs:sec><hp:p><hp:run><hp:polygon id="1_1"><hp:pt x="0" y="0"/></hp:polygon></hp:run></hp:p></hs:sec>`;
      expect(isValidOpenPathHwpx(mutatedBrokenOpenPathHwpx)).toBe(false);

      const mutatedBrokenDonutElem = { type: "oval" };
      expect(isValidDonutArcElement(mutatedBrokenDonutElem)).toBe(false);
    });

    it("should synthesize missing absoluteBoundingBox for Auto-Layout children and keep stacked section CANVAS frames on a single page", () => {
      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser();

      // 1. Nested Auto-Layout without child absoluteBoundingBox
      const nestedAutoPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/real-world/sample-b-nested-autolayout.json");
      const nestedDoc = parser.parse(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(nestedAutoPath, "utf-8")));
      expect(nestedDoc.pages.length).toBe(1);
      const nestedTexts = nestedDoc.pages[0].elements
        .filter((e) => e.type === "text")
        .map((e) => e.textRuns?.map((r) => r.text).join("") || "");
      expect(nestedTexts).toContain("주요 서비스 모듈 현황 (Nested Auto Layout)");
      expect(nestedTexts).toContain("보안 등급 1등급");
      expect(nestedTexts).toContain("데이터 격리 및 로컬 암호화");

      // 2. Stacked section CANVAS with top-level TEXT/LINE and Auto-Layout cards
      const cardGridPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/real-world/sample-e2e-card-grid.json");
      const cardGridDoc = parser.parse(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cardGridPath, "utf-8")));
      expect(cardGridDoc.pages.length).toBe(1);
      expect(cardGridDoc.pages[0].elements.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(11);
      const cardTexts = cardGridDoc.pages[0].elements
        .filter((e) => e.type === "text")
        .map((e) => e.textRuns?.map((r) => r.text).join("") || "");
      expect(cardTexts).toContain("주요 서비스 및 제품 포트폴리오");
      expect(cardTexts).toContain("스타터 (Starter)");
    });
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/phase17-quality-integration.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../src/canvas/parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { CanvasDocument, CanvasElement } from "../src/canvas/types.js";

describe("Phase 17 Quality & Integration Test Suite", () => {
  // =========================================================================
  // 1. Native HWPX Line Rendering for LINE Elements
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Track A: HWPX Native Line Rendering", () => {
    it("should render horizontal/vertical divider lines as canonical thin filled <hp:rect> in HWPX", async () => {
      const lineDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Divider Line Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "div_1",
                name: "Horizontal Divider",
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 100, width: 400, height: 1 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { strokeColor: "#336699", strokeWidth: 2 },
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(lineDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Verify canonical thin filled <hp:rect> matching authentic Hancom reference documents
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:winBrush faceColor="#336699"');
      expect(secXml).toMatch(/<hp:sz width="40000" height="200"/);
      expect(secXml).toMatch(/<hp:lineShape color="#000000" width="0" style="NONE"/);
    });

    it("should render dashed or diagonal lines as native <hp:line> with startPt and endPt in HWPX", async () => {
      const dashedLineDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Native Dashed Line Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "div_dashed",
                name: "Dashed Line",
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 100, width: 400, height: 2 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { strokeColor: "#3B82F6", strokeWidth: 2, strokeDashed: true },
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(dashedLineDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Verify native <hp:line> with startPt and endPt conforming to OWPML LineType
      const lineMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:line\b[\s\S]*?<\/hp:line>/);
      expect(lineMatch).toBeDefined();
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('isReverseHV="0"');
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('color="#3B82F6"');
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('style="DASH"');
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('<hc:startPt x="0" y="0"/>');
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('<hc:endPt x="40000" y="200"/>');
    });

    it("should strictly cap rounded rectangle ratio to 50 for pills and semi-circles", async () => {
      const pillDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Pill Shape Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "pill_1",
                name: "Badge Pill",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 120, height: 40 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#007ACC", cornerRadius: 50 }, // Corner radius exceeds minDim / 2 (20)
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(pillDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const rectMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:rect\b[^>]*\bratio="(\d+)"/);
      expect(rectMatch).toBeDefined();
      const ratio = parseInt(rectMatch![1], 10);
      expect(ratio).toBe(50); // Capped at exactly 50 per KS X 6101 schema
    });

    it("should emit 75 HWPU/px image dimensions and both hp:imgDim and hp:inImgDim", async () => {
      // 100x50 PNG sample bytes
      const pngHeader = Buffer.from([
        0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a, // PNG magic
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d, // IHDR length (13)
        0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52, // IHDR
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // width = 100 px
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x32, // height = 50 px
        0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, // 8-bit truecolor
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, // CRC placeholder
      ]);

      const imgDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Image Dimension Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "img_dim_test",
                name: "Test Image",
                type: "image",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 50 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                imageRef: "res_test_img.png",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {
          "res_test_img.png": {
            id: "res_test_img.png",
            name: "res_test_img.png",
            mimeType: "image/png",
            data: pngHeader,
          },
        },
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(imgDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // 100 px * 75 = 7500 HWPU; 50 px * 75 = 3750 HWPU
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:imgDim dimwidth="7500" dimheight="3750"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('binaryItemIDRef="BIN0001"');
      // Phase 20 OWPML Schema Fix: ensure spurious inImgDim and binRef are not emitted
      expect(secXml).not.toContain('<hp:inImgDim');
      expect(secXml).not.toContain('binRef="BIN0001"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:imgRect>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:pt1 x="10000" y="0"/>');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. Track B: Mixed Orientation & Multi-Page Sections
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Track B: Mixed Orientation Multi-Page Documents", () => {
    it("should generate proper section break in DOCX when page orientations differ", async () => {
      const mixedDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Mixed Orientation Document",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
            elements: [
              {
                id: "p1_text",
                name: "Page 1 Portrait",
                type: "text",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 50 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                textRuns: [{ text: "Portrait Page 1", fontFamily: "Calibri", fontSizePt: 12 }],
              },
            ],
          },
          {
            pageNumber: 2,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 841.89, height: 595.28 },
            backgroundColor: "#F9F9F9",
            elements: [
              {
                id: "p2_text",
                name: "Page 2 Landscape",
                type: "text",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 300, height: 50 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                textRuns: [{ text: "Landscape Page 2", fontFamily: "Calibri", fontSizePt: 12 }],
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      // Verify Page 1 paragraph has inline sectPr with w:type="nextPage" and portrait size
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:type w:val="nextPage"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('w:w="11906" w:h="16838" w:orient="portrait"');

      // Verify final document sectPr has landscape size
      expect(docXml).toContain('w:w="16838" w:h="11906" w:orient="landscape"');
    });

    it("should generate per-section hp:secPr in HWPX when page orientations differ", async () => {
      const mixedDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Mixed Orientation Document HWPX",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
            elements: [],
          },
          {
            pageNumber: 2,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 841.89, height: 595.28 },
            backgroundColor: "#F0F0F0",
            elements: [],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const secPrMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:secPr\b/g);
      expect(secPrMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(secPrMatches!.length).toBe(2);

      // Page 1 is Portrait (WIDELY), Page 2 is Landscape (NARROWLY), with portrait-base dimensions
      expect(secXml).toContain('landscape="WIDELY" width="59528" height="84189"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('landscape="NARROWLY" width="59528" height="84189"');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 3. Real-World Fixture Conversion
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Track B: Real-World Fixture Integrity", () => {
    it("should convert real-world proposal doc fixture cleanly into DOCX and HWPX", async () => {
      const fixturePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/real-world/sample-a-proposal-doc.json");
      expect(fs.existsSync(fixturePath)).toBe(true);

      const rawJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(fixturePath, "utf-8"));
      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ titleOverride: "Real World Proposal" });
      const doc = parser.parse(rawJson);

      expect(doc.pages.length).toBe(1);
      expect(doc.pages[0].elements.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6);
      const textElements = doc.pages[0].elements.filter((e) => e.type === "text");
      expect(textElements.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);

      // Render DOCX
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      expect(docxBuf.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docxXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(docxXml).toContain("2026 차세대 엔터프라이즈 AI 자동화 구축 제안서");
      expect(docxXml).toContain("1. 핵심 기대 효과 및 도입 타당성");

      // Render HWPX
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      expect(hwpxBuf.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const hwpxSecXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(hwpxSecXml).toContain("2026 차세대 엔터프라이즈 AI 자동화 구축 제안서");
      expect(hwpxSecXml).toContain("1. 핵심 기대 효과 및 도입 타당성");
    });
  });
});



================================================================================
FILE: tests/phase16-1-remediation.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../src/canvas/parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { CanvasDocument, CanvasElement } from "../src/canvas/types.js";

describe("Phase 16.1 Independent Audit Findings Remediation Tests", () => {
  // =========================================================================
  // 1. P0: Real Figma Input Landscape Handling (complex-nested-dashboard)
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P0: Real Figma Input Landscape Handling", () => {
    it("should dynamically detect landscape orientation and dimensions from top-level Figma frame", async () => {
      const samplePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/synthetic-figma/complex-nested-dashboard.json");
      const rawJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(samplePath, "utf-8"));

      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ titleOverride: "Dashboard Landscape" });
      const doc = parser.parse(rawJson);

      // Frame is 842 x 595 (A4 landscape ratio)
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeGreaterThan(doc.pageSize.heightPt);
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeCloseTo(841.89, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.orientation).toBe("landscape");

      // Verify DOCX Output has landscape orientation and matching twip dimensions
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      const pgSzMatch = docXml.match(/<w:pgSz\b[^>]*\/?>/);
      expect(pgSzMatch).toBeDefined();
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:orient="landscape"');
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:w="16838"');
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:h="11906"');

      // Verify HWPX Output has WIDELY and matching HWPU dimensions
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const pagePrMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:pagePr\b[^>]*\/?>/);
      expect(pagePrMatch).toBeDefined();
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('landscape="NARROWLY"');
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('width="59528"');
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('height="84189"');
    });

    it("should preserve portrait A4 dimensions for portrait documents (multi-page-report)", () => {
      const samplePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/synthetic-figma/multi-page-report.json");
      const rawJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(samplePath, "utf-8"));

      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ titleOverride: "Multi-page Portrait" });
      const doc = parser.parse(rawJson);

      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeGreaterThan(doc.pageSize.widthPt);
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeCloseTo(841.89, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.orientation).toBe("portrait");
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. P1: HWPX Multi-Subpath and Open Path Separation
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: HWPX Multi-Subpath and Open Path", () => {
    it("should emit multiple disconnected subpaths as separate HWPX polygons", async () => {
      const compoundDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Compound Subpath Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "vec1",
                name: "Two Boxes",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#00FF00", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1 },
                rawSvgPath: "M 0 0 L 50 0 L 50 50 L 0 50 Z M 100 0 L 150 0 L 150 50 L 100 50 Z",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(compoundDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const polygonMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:polygon\b/g);
      expect(polygonMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(polygonMatches!.length).toBe(2);
    });

    it("should preserve open paths (like checkmarks) without closing lines or spurious fills", async () => {
      const checkmarkDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Checkmark Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "check1",
                name: "Checkmark",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { strokeColor: "#0000FF", strokeWidth: 2 },
                rawSvgPath: "M 10 50 L 40 80 L 90 20",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(checkmarkDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Open paths must NOT be emitted as <hp:polygon> (which auto-closes in Hancom)
      expect(secXml).not.toContain("<hp:polygon");
      const lineMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:line\b/g);
      expect(lineMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(lineMatches!.length).toBe(2);
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:startPt x="0" y="5000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:endPt x="3750" y="10000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:endPt x="10000" y="0"/>');
    });

    it("should distinguish closed vs open subpaths in mixed multi-contour paths", async () => {
      const mixedDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Mixed Path Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "mix1",
                name: "Circle and Line",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 200 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#FF0000", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1 },
                // Closed outer box + open inner slash
                rawSvgPath: "M 0 0 L 100 0 L 100 100 L 0 100 Z M 20 20 L 80 80",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Closed box is emitted as <hp:polygon> with fill; open slash is emitted as <hp:line> without fill
      const polygonMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:polygon\b/g);
      expect(polygonMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(polygonMatches!.length).toBe(1);

      const lineMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:line\b/g);
      expect(lineMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(lineMatches!.length).toBe(1);

      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:winBrush faceColor="#FF0000"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:startPt x="4000" y="4000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:endPt x="16000" y="16000"/>');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 3. P1: Image FILL Crop Verification
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: Image FILL Crop Verification", () => {
    const widePngBytes = new Uint8Array([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d,
      0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xc8, // width = 200
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // height = 100
      0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    ]);

    const tallPngBytes = new Uint8Array([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d,
      0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // width = 100
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xc8, // height = 200
      0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    ]);

    it("should calculate correct imgClip bounds for horizontal overflow in HWPX (left & right crop)", async () => {
      const imageDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Crop Test Wide",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "img1",
                name: "Wide Image in Square Box",
                type: "image",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                imageRef: "sample_wide.png",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {
          "sample_wide.png": {
            id: "sample_wide.png",
            name: "sample_wide.png",
            mimeType: "image/png",
            data: widePngBytes,
          },
        },
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(imageDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const imgClipMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:imgClip\s+([^>]+)\/>/);
      expect(imgClipMatch).toBeDefined();
      const clipAttrs = imgClipMatch![1];

      const leftVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/left="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      const rightVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/right="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      expect(leftVal).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(rightVal).toBeLessThan(20000);
    });

    it("should calculate correct imgClip bounds for vertical overflow in HWPX (top & bottom crop)", async () => {
      const imageDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Crop Test Tall",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "img2",
                name: "Tall Image in Square Box",
                type: "image",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                imageRef: "sample_tall.png",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {
          "sample_tall.png": {
            id: "sample_tall.png",
            name: "sample_tall.png",
            mimeType: "image/png",
            data: tallPngBytes,
          },
        },
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(imageDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const imgClipMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:imgClip\s+([^>]+)\/>/);
      expect(imgClipMatch).toBeDefined();
      const clipAttrs = imgClipMatch![1];

      const topVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/top="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      const bottomVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/bottom="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      expect(topVal).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(bottomVal).toBeLessThan(20000);
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 4. P1: Text Vertical Alignment F-12
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: Text Vertical Alignment F-12", () => {
    it("should preserve TOP, CENTER, BOTTOM alignment across Canvas IR, DOCX, and HWPX", async () => {
      const figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
      const textTop = {
        id: "text:top",
        name: "Top Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Top Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "TOP" },
      };
      const textCenter = {
        id: "text:center",
        name: "Center Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Center Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 150, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "CENTER" },
      };
      const textBottom = {
        id: "text:bottom",
        name: "Bottom Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Bottom Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 250, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "BOTTOM" },
      };

      const doc = figmaParser.parse({
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Vertical Align Comparison",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [textTop, textCenter, textBottom],
      } as any);

      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[0].verticalAlign).toBe("top");
      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[1].verticalAlign).toBe("center");
      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[2].verticalAlign).toBe("bottom");

      // Verify DOCX VML emits exact v-text-anchor tags
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:top");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:middle");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:bottom");

      // Verify HWPX emits exact vertAlign attributes
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="TOP"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="CENTER"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="BOTTOM"');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 5. P2: HWPX Rounded Corners F-13
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P2: HWPX Rounded Corners F-13", () => {
    it("should compute accurate ratio for cornerRadius=0, 8, 24 and cards with stroke & fill", async () => {
      const cardsDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Corner Radius Scale Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "c0",
                name: "Sharp Rect (r=0)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#FFFFFF", cornerRadius: 0 },
              },
              {
                id: "c8",
                name: "Round Card (r=8)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 160, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 2,
                style: { fillColor: "#F0F0F0", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1, cornerRadius: 8 },
              },
              {
                id: "c24",
                name: "Round Card (r=24)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 270, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 3,
                style: { fillColor: "#E0E0E0", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1, cornerRadius: 24 },
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(cardsDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const rectMatches = Array.from(secXml.matchAll(/<hp:rect\b[^>]*\bratio="(\d+)"/g));
      expect(rectMatches.length).toBe(3);

      const ratio0 = parseInt(rectMatches[0][1], 10);
      const ratio8 = parseInt(rectMatches[1][1], 10);
      const ratio24 = parseInt(rectMatches[2][1], 10);

      expect(ratio0).toBe(0);
      expect(ratio8).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(ratio24).toBeGreaterThan(ratio8);
    });
  });
});

FILE: tests/phase16-h2-remediation.test.ts
